The ghost-trail's per-instance cutout threshold is held **uniform across the entire TRAVERSING duration** — 3 cubes visible at consistent half-unit object-space spacing, no scale-up, no late-flight emphasis. **Speed is communicated through forward perspective foreshortening only** — a math-only Pillar 5 truth (faster waves traverse `SPAWN_PLANE_Z_OFFSET_M` in less time, so the screen-space rate of approach is higher; this is a direct projection of the simulation state, not a separate cue layered on top). **The trail itself does NOT differentially encode velocity at PEAK vs OPENER tiers**: the ghost cubes are anchored in object-space at fixed half-unit intervals behind the wave mass and translate rigidly with it, so a fast wave and a slow wave produce the same world-space trail geometry at any given instant — only the rate at which that geometry approaches the camera differs (R5 B5 — the R4 "more spread-out ghost cubes" rationale was geometrically false at fixed object-space spacing; corrected to honest direction-only role). The trail communicates trajectory direction and "wave-in-motion" identity; it does NOT carry a differential-velocity signal. If a future design iteration wants a trail-borne velocity cue, the trail's offset spacing would need to be a function of `ForwardVelocityMs` (parameterized through `PerInstanceCustomData` per ISMC pattern); deferred to post-MVP Visual Polish and requires Pillar 5 design test (cue must correspond exactly to simulation state if learnable). **R8 perf-analyst R-1 — slot count drift correction**: the prior R5-era prose referenced a `PerInstanceCustomData[3]` channel on the wave-mass ISMC for TrailAlpha; this is OBSOLETE post-R7 B9. The wave-mass ISMC has exactly 3 PerInstanceCustomData slots (0=LeanCharge, 1=NearMissFlash, 2=VoxelDissolve — see R1 RC-G G-2 binding); there is no [3] slot on the wave-mass ISMC. TrailAlpha now lives on the separate trail-cube ISMC at PerInstanceCustomData[0] (per R7 B9 trail-cube ISMC separation). No in-flight scale modulation is used in the current spec; if a future iteration adds a trail-borne velocity cue (deferred to post-MVP Visual Polish), the trail-cube ISMC's PerInstanceCustomData layout would be extended on that ISMC, not on the wave-mass ISMC.

**Pillar 5 design-test addition (R3 CD adjudication #1, made explicit here).** Any future "approach intensity" or "speed-up" cue proposal MUST satisfy: *if the player can learn the cue and time dodges to it, the cue must correspond exactly to simulation state.* Perspective foreshortening passes (math = simulation). Per-instance ramps that don't reflect a simulation change fail. Defer math-only perspective-projection growth treatments to post-MVP Visual Polish; do not reintroduce perception-deception cues.

**LANDED visual (one-tick hit vs near-miss vs clean-miss).** The wave does NOT shatter or fragment on landing — the death moment is owned by the Collision GDD and PM's freeze behavior. Wave-side differentiation:

- **Hit:** mass continues forward past player plane without special effect; it arrived, it landed, the Collision GDD fires the death event. The wave proceeds to DESPAWNING. No additional wave-side visual.
- **Near-miss (R4 RC-R3-6 — duration promoted to registry):** a chromatic edge flash — the outermost voxel faces on the side facing the player's source lane briefly invert to a high-saturation cool highlight (approx. blue-white, 100% saturation, 80% brightness) for exactly `NEAR_MISS_FLASH_DURATION_S = 0.066s` (registry constant — see `design/registry/entities.yaml`; ≈4 frames at 60Hz, ≈2 frames at 30Hz, ≈8 frames at 120Hz), then drop to normal before the mass exits the player plane. **Duration is TIME-BASED, not frame-count** — R3 3-way convergence (game-d + level-d + art-d) found the prior 2-frame spec (33ms at 60fps) was below the 50–80ms reliable conscious-perception threshold for a peripheral chromatic event; at 30fps the 2-frame spec collapsed to 66ms (passing) but at 120Hz collapsed to 16ms (failing). Time-based `0.066s` (66ms) sits at the threshold floor at any frame rate. Implemented as a `NearMissEdgeFlash` material scalar routed through **`PerInstanceCustomData[1]`** (per R1 RC-G G-2): set to 1.0 on near-miss entry; decayed to 0.0 over `NEAR_MISS_FLASH_DURATION_S` via a frame-rate-independent time accumulator (`flash_elapsed_s += DeltaTime`; `intensity = max(0, 1.0 − flash_elapsed_s / NEAR_MISS_FLASH_DURATION_S)`). The shader reads this scalar to drive the cool-highlight blend on the source-lane-facing voxel edge faces. This effect composites independently of PM's Y-dip animation and remains in the single batched ISMC draw call. Note: this duration outlives the LANDED hold (`WAVE_DESPAWN_HOLD_S = 0.15s > NEAR_MISS_FLASH_DURATION_S = 0.066s`), so the flash completes before DESPAWNING; if `WAVE_DESPAWN_HOLD_S` is ever tightened below `NEAR_MISS_FLASH_DURATION_S`, the flash is truncated — knob-interaction note (see Tuning Knobs).
- **Clean-miss:** no differentiation. The mass passes through without any special visual. The trail fades naturally per despawn.

**DESPAWNING follow-through (WAVE_DESPAWN_HOLD_S = 0.15s hold then cleanup).** After LANDED, the mass continues forward in world space past the player plane at `forward_velocity_ms` during the hold. At DESPAWNING entry, the mass dissolves via a per-instance alpha-test threshold ramp over 0.10s (`VoxelDissolveFade` scalar routed through **`PerInstanceCustomData[2]`** per R1 RC-G G-2: cutout dissolve, not translucent fade — mobile tile-GPU consistent). Individual voxels in the cluster DO NOT scatter or physically separate — the cluster holds its shape and dissolves uniformly via the cutout-threshold animation. No shattering, no burst expansion, no debris. The anti-pillar constraint is binding: dissolve only, no chaotic secondary effects.

### Near-Miss Visual Juice (cross-system with PM)

**Wave-side responsibility.** Pull-Wave detects the near-miss (Rule 11) and calls `PM.TriggerNearMissBeat()`. The avatar Y-dip is PM's domain. Pull-Wave additionally owns a wave-side response: the `NearMissEdgeFlash` described above (a `NEAR_MISS_FLASH_DURATION_S = 0.066s` cool-highlight pulse on the source-lane-facing voxel edge faces; R5 B1 — was stale "2-frame" wording from before R4 RC-R3-6 promoted duration to a time-based registry constant). This is the wave's acknowledgment that it passed through occupied space.

**Pillar 5 binding.** The player must be able to point to the exact moment the wave passed through the lane they just vacated. The edge flash provides a wave-side visual reference point independent of the avatar dip. The two signals — avatar dip (PM-owned) and edge flash (Pull-Wave-owned) — fire simultaneously at the LANDED tick and are visible as distinct effects: the avatar's body flinches inward while the wave's edge face briefly lights up in the lane the player left. Together they answer "that was close" from both directions.

**Scale and mobile readability.** The edge flash must be visible at 6-inch mobile screen distance. The affected voxel faces are the full lateral cross-section of the mass facing `source_lane` — typically 2–3 voxel faces wide and 2 faces tall. The cool-highlight color (blue-white at 80% brightness) is chosen to maximally contrast with the base iron-gray material on a warm-toned track background. Art bible authoring will confirm the exact color values against the finalized track palette.

**Audio boundary.** All audio response to `OnNearMiss` is the Audio Controller's domain. This section specifies visual only.

## UI Requirements

Pull-Wave has no UI surface — no HUD widget, no on-screen wave indicator, no "incoming wave" warning text, no targeting reticle. This is a Pillar 4 ("Surreal in Motion, Not in Menus") binding: the player reads the wave through its own behavior — the lean, the curve, the approach — not through UI chrome.

**Forbidden HUD elements** (recorded here so In-Run HUD GDD inherits the prohibition):

- Wave-count counter
- Per-wave targeting indicators or arrows
- "Barrage incoming" warning
- Lane occupancy indicators
- Approach-distance meter or threat-radius visualization

The wave's voxel mass and its lean ARE the UI of this system. Adding chrome would short-circuit the Pillar 2 read-and-react loop the game is built around.

## Acceptance Criteria

All ACs below are **Logic** classification unless explicitly marked otherwise. Default gate level **BLOCKING**. AC-PW-22a is ADVISORY (manual on-device measurement). AC-PW-PILLAR-2-BARRAGE-SPATIAL-K and AC-PW-SLIP-TWEEN-CONSISTENCY are static analysis checks (cook-time/tooling-time, not runtime).

### Build-Configuration Scoping Convention

ACs that validate `check()` assertions (e.g., AC-PW-27, AC-PW-30) target **Shipping or Test build configuration** for their observable "process still alive" assertions. In these configurations `check()` is a no-op (compiled out) and the parallel runtime guard — `if (!cond) { UE_LOG(LogPullWave, Error, ...); return; }` — is the sole active code path. In Debug/Development builds, `check()` fires first and aborts; that path is verified by crash-log review or expect-death subprocess test. No new compile-time flags required.

### Test Infrastructure Dependencies

- **Seam 7 (R2 Cluster F extended)**: `IRSMTimeStateProvider` + `FRSMTestStub` — RSM state/time injection. R2 extension adds: `OnPausedChanged` multicast delegate (fires on `SetIsPaused()` when `current_state == RUNNING` and value changes); `SetCurrentStateWithPauseClear(ERunState)` helper for the PAUSED→ABORTED same-tick transition WITHOUT firing `OnPausedChanged` (CF-A3 contract). Required for AC-PW-16 (pause-freeze drive), AC-PW-17a/b (EC-STALE-PAUSE-ABORTED no-teleport path). See `docs/architecture/platform-seam-interfaces.md` Seam 7.
- **Seam 11**: `ICurveProvider` + `FCurveProviderTestStub` — `LateralOffsetCurve` injection with specific or NaN return values.
- **Seam 12 (R7 Pull-Wave authoring; R2 Cluster D cleanup)**: `IPlayerMovementProvider` + `FPlayerMovementTestStub` — PM state injection without instantiating real PM. Required API used by Pull-Wave ACs post-R1: `SetCurrentLane`, `SetTargetLane`, `SetMovementState`, `GetNearMissBeatCount()`. R1 RC-A removed `OnSlipMidpoint` subscription; R2 Cluster D stripped the stub's `FireSlipMidpoint` method (no current consumer). See `docs/architecture/platform-seam-interfaces.md` Seam 12.
- **Seam 13 (R7 Pull-Wave authoring; R2 Cluster F extended)**: `IWaveSpawnerCallback` + `FWaveSpawnerCallbackTestStub` — captures `OnWaveDespawned` broadcasts + collision-unregister-before-broadcast ordering invariant. R2 extension adds: `HandleWaveHit(WaveId, TargetLane, SourceLane)` and `HandleNearMiss(WaveId, TargetLane, SlippedFromLane)` delegate-listener methods for binding to Pull-Wave's `OnWaveHit` / `OnNearMiss` multicast delegates during test setup; `GetWaveHitCount()`, `GetNearMissCount()` counters; `GetEventLogInOrder()` unified ordered event log across all event types for AC-PW-10 ordering assertions. Full required API: `GetDespawnEventCount`, `GetLastDespawnReason`, `GetLastFinalLane`, `WasCollisionUnregisteredBeforeBroadcast`, `GetWaveHitCount`, `GetNearMissCount`, `GetEventLogInOrder`. See `docs/architecture/platform-seam-interfaces.md` Seam 13.
- **Pull-Wave instance test accessor (CF-A1 — R4 RC-R3-3 redesign to state-aware struct)**: `TraverseElapsedS` is an internal `FPullWaveInstanceState` field. Exposed via a `#if !UE_BUILD_SHIPPING` test accessor on `FPullWaveSubsystem` that returns a **state-aware struct** (NOT a `-1.0f` sentinel — the R3 RC-R3-3 finding was that the sentinel conflated "WaveId not found" with "wave found but not in TRAVERSING," letting AC-PW-16's freeze assertion pass on a pause-flush bug that wrongly transitioned the wave out of TRAVERSING during the freeze window):

  ```cpp
  // R4 RC-R3-3 — state-aware test accessor; replaces the prior -1.0f sentinel-on-not-found pattern with
  // a state-aware struct. R7 B5 — ElapsedS sentinel for undefined-state cases changed 0.0f → -1.0f so an
  // unguarded read (test author forgets to assert State == TRAVERSING) sees a visibly-wrong value
  // rather than a value coinciding with the "accumulator at zero" valid TRAVERSING-entry state.
  struct FTraverseElapsedQuery {
      bool             bWaveFound;      // false ⇔ no wave with this WaveId in the active list
      EPullWaveState   State;           // valid only when bWaveFound == true
      float            ElapsedS;        // valid only when bWaveFound == true AND State == TRAVERSING or LANDED;
                                        // -1.0f when wave found but State ∈ {SPAWNED, LEANING, DESPAWNING}
                                        // (R7 B5 — sentinel changed from 0.0f to -1.0f to make unguarded
                                        //  reads visibly fail rather than coincide with the legitimate
                                        //  TRAVERSING-entry value 0.0f. Test authors omitting the State
                                        //  assertion now see a negative ElapsedS — outside the [0, TravelDurationS]
                                        //  valid range — and the assertion fails loudly. TraverseElapsedS itself
                                        //  remains undefined in those states per FPullWaveInstanceState field comment;
                                        //  the accessor synthesizes -1.0f rather than read the undefined field value.)
  };
  FTraverseElapsedQuery GetTraverseElapsedS_ForTest(int32 WaveId) const;
  ```

  Required for AC-PW-12, AC-PW-16, AC-PW-17b (accumulator-level no-teleport assertions). Tests MUST assert `bWaveFound` AND `State` explicitly before reading `ElapsedS` — a passing assertion requires the wave to BE in the expected state, closing the false-positive trap (a pause-flush bug routing the wave to DESPAWNING during the 20-tick freeze window now FAILS both the `State == TRAVERSING` check AND `ElapsedS >= 0.0f` because the sentinel `-1.0f` is outside the valid range). Not an injectable seam; if Death Replay or Telemetry later need per-instance read from outside the module, migrate to a new `IPullWaveInstanceObserver` seam (deferred).
- **Pull-Wave tick harness DeltaTime injection (CF-A2)**: The tick harness driving AC-PW-16 (20-tick pause-freeze assertion) and AC-PW-17b (N-tick arithmetic assertion) MUST accept injected `DeltaTime` per tick — the harness must NOT read real wall-clock elapsed time. If the existing harness passes a real `float DeltaTime` from the engine tick, replace it with a test-controlled `float InjectedDeltaTime` parameter. Without injected delta, the 20-tick and N-tick assertions in AC-PW-16/17b are nondeterministic across different hardware and build configurations.
- **FTestLogCapture** — `FOutputDevice` subclass capturing log lines per category/verbosity.
- Canonical test lateral offset curve fixture: `Content/Test/Fixtures/PullWave/CanonicalLateralOffsetCurve_TestFixture.uasset` — linear curve returning `t_norm` (endpoints 0.0→1.0 exactly). Cooked into non-shipping test builds only.
- Float epsilon: `1e-5` for `t_norm`, `world_x`, `world_z` (R3 editorial fix: `1e-9` is below IEEE 754 single-precision epsilon (~1.19e-7) — assertions at that tolerance are nondeterministic on accumulator paths). `CURVE_ENDPOINT_TOLERANCE = 0.005` for endpoint assertions.

Seams 8, 9, 10 are NOT applicable to Pull-Wave (no DPC-style snapshot-consumer contract, no abort delegate, no Wave Spawner pool metadata query).

---

### Formula Correctness

- **AC-PW-01 (R4 — Cluster-A residue sweep)** — **GIVEN** wave in TRAVERSING with `ForwardVelocityMs = 7.5`, `SPAWN_PLANE_Z_OFFSET_M = 15.0` (so `TravelDurationS = 2.0`), accumulator `TraverseElapsedS = 1.2` (driven via the AC-PW-12 tick-order contract: read accumulator → evaluate F-TRAJ-TNORM → increment, with 1.2s of unpaused TRAVERSING DeltaTime delivered through Seam 7 / CF-A2 injected delta), **WHEN** F-TRAJ-TNORM evaluates, **THEN** `|t_norm − 0.6| < 1e-5` — canonical example under the post-Cluster-A accumulator semantics. (The R1-draft `GameTime`/`SpawnTimeS`/`LeanDurationS` GIVEN was retired with F-TRAJ-TNORM's wall-clock form per R2 Cluster A; this AC's GIVEN is now constructible through the Seam 7 / CF-A1 / CF-A2 test infrastructure.)
- **AC-PW-02 (R4 — Cluster-A residue sweep)** — **GIVEN** wave in LEANING (TRAVERSING not yet entered; accumulator never initialized — `FTraverseElapsedQuery.bWaveFound = true`, `State = LEANING`, `ElapsedS` undefined), **WHEN** F-TRAJ-TNORM is evaluated under the AC's "valid only when State == TRAVERSING or LANDED" precondition (see F-TRAJ-TNORM Valid-only-when clause), **THEN** the formula is NOT evaluated for LEANING waves; observable equivalent: world_z remains at `SpawnPlaneZ` (no F-TRAJ-FORWARD evaluation either, since forward motion gates on TRAVERSING). Cross-reference: AC-PW-12 verifies the LEANING→TRAVERSING boundary t_norm=0.0 reading directly. (The R1-draft "lower clamp" assertion is structurally satisfied by the precondition gate rather than a runtime clamp; documented here to retain AC-ID continuity.)
- **AC-PW-03 (R4 — Cluster-A residue sweep)** — **GIVEN** wave in LANDED with accumulator `TraverseElapsedS = TravelDurationS` (clamped at LANDED entry per `FPullWaveInstanceState.TraverseElapsedS` field comment), and `LandedHoldElapsedS ∈ [0, WAVE_DESPAWN_HOLD_S]`, **WHEN** F-TRAJ-TNORM evaluates (LANDED is in F-TRAJ-TNORM's Valid-only-when set), **THEN** `t_norm == 1.0` (upper clamp; reachable because `TraverseElapsedS / TravelDurationS = 1.0` exactly under the explicit cap, with the output clamp as defensive fallback) AND F-TRAJ-FORWARD yields `world_z == PLAYER_PLANE_Z == 0.0` (algebraic identity, not floating-point coincidence). Held throughout the ≈9-tick LANDED hold at 60 fps; verified via `GetTraverseElapsedS_ForTest(WaveId)` returning `{bWaveFound=true, State=LANDED, ElapsedS=TravelDurationS}` (CF-A1 state-aware struct).
- **AC-PW-04** — **GIVEN** `SourceLane = 4`, `TargetLane = 1`, `LANE_WIDTH_M = 1.0`, `NUM_LANES = 5`, `t_norm = 0.6`, `FCurveProviderTestStub` returns `0.7` at `t_norm = 0.6` (snapshot evaluator returns same), **WHEN** F-TRAJ-LATERAL evaluates, **THEN** `|world_x − (−0.1)| < 1e-5` — canonical 5-lane example: `source_lane_x = 4.0 − 2.0 = 2.0`; `lateral_offset = 0.7 × (1 − 4) × 1.0 = −2.1`; `world_x = 2.0 + (−2.1) = −0.1`.
- **AC-PW-05** — **GIVEN** `SourceLane == TargetLane = 2`, any `t_norm ∈ [0.0, 1.0]`, **WHEN** F-TRAJ-LATERAL evaluates at `t_norm ∈ {0.0, 0.5, 1.0}`, **THEN** `|world_x − source_lane_x| < 1e-5` on all three with `source_lane_x = 0.0` (straight-forward wave at center lane; valid pattern; tier-0 `lean_magnitude_tier`).
- **AC-PW-06** — **GIVEN** `t_norm = 0.0` and canonical curve snapshot (`Samples[0] = 0.0`), **WHEN** F-TRAJ-LATERAL evaluates, **THEN** `|world_x − source_lane_x| < 1e-5`.
- **AC-PW-07** — **GIVEN** `t_norm = 1.0`, `SourceLane = 0`, `TargetLane = 4`, `NUM_LANES = 5`, **WHEN** F-TRAJ-LATERAL evaluates, **THEN** `|world_x − 2.0| < 1e-5` (lands at rightmost lane center; tier-4 `lean_magnitude_tier`).
- **AC-PW-08** — **GIVEN** `SpawnPlaneZ = 15.0`, `t_norm = 0.6`, `TravelDurationS = 2.0`, `ForwardVelocityMs = 7.5`, **WHEN** F-TRAJ-FORWARD evaluates, **THEN** `|world_z − 6.0| < 1e-5`. Plus: `t_norm = 0.0 → world_z = 15.0`; `t_norm = 1.0 → world_z = 0.0` (algebraic identity; exact, no tolerance).
- **AC-PW-09** — **GIVEN** `SPAWN_PLANE_Z_OFFSET_M = 15.0`, `ForwardVelocityMs = 4.0`, **WHEN** F-TRAVERSE-DURATION evaluates, **THEN** `|TravelDurationS − 3.75| < 1e-5`. At max velocity 18.0: `|TravelDurationS − 0.833…| < 1e-5`.
- **AC-PW-10 (Determinism — Rule 14; R6 B7 — same-tick multi-admission coverage extended; R7 B11 — admit-tick visit-order assertion struck because at admit tick T both waves are SPAWNED and emit zero Seam 13 events; the iteration-order invariant is now enforced at design layer by AC-PW-22b pattern 11 static FORBID grep on `TArray::Insert` plus pattern 9 ALLOW grep on `RemoveAt`)** — **GIVEN** two simulations spawning identical wave sequences in identical wave_id order with identical `(WaveId, SourceLane, TargetLane, CurveSnapshot, ForwardVelocityMs, LeanDurationS, SpawnTimeS)` per wave and an identical 100-tick game-time sequence; **the spawn schedule MUST include at least one tick on which two or more waves are admitted in the same tick** (e.g., a barrage onset cluster at any tick T where Wave Spawner calls `Construct()` for `wave_id = N` and `wave_id = N+1` within the same tick), **WHEN** active-wave iteration runs in `WaveId ASCENDING` order each tick AND `(world_x, world_z, t_norm, state)` snapshots are compared tick-by-tick across both simulations for each `WaveId`, **THEN** all four fields compare equal (bit-identical floats) on every tick for every wave. Additionally: broadcast order of `OnWaveHit` / `OnNearMiss` / `OnWaveDespawned` across two waves landing in the same tick is `WaveId ASCENDING` in both simulations (verified by Seam 13 stub event-order recording — the visible same-tick downstream-event ordering proxy IS observable through the declared seams, unlike the admit-tick SPAWNED-state iteration order which is not). **R7 B11 rationale (struck-clause replacement)**: at admit tick T, both `wave_id = N` and `wave_id = N+1` are in SPAWNED state for one tick (Rule 3 state lifecycle); SPAWNED emits zero Seam 13 events (no `WaveHit`, `NearMiss`, `Despawned`, `CollisionUnregistered`, or `TelegraphUnregistered` records are written to `GetEventLogInOrder()` during SPAWNED-state processing). The R6 B7 "active-wave list iteration immediately following admission must visit `wave_id = N` before `wave_id = N+1`" assertion has no observable witness at tick T — two simulations using the same buggy `TArray::Insert(NewState, 0)` produce identical wrong orderings that compare equal under the bit-identical assertion, vacuously satisfying the comparative test. The actual `TArray::Add` (append-to-tail) binding is enforced by **AC-PW-22b pattern 11** FORBID grep `TArray::Insert\b|\.Insert\s*\(` on the active-wave-list source path — a design-layer static guard sufficient to prevent the bug class. The runtime visit-order assertion is therefore retired in favor of the static guard. The same-tick admission requirement in the GIVEN above is preserved so the bit-identical comparison across the FIRST tick on which two waves co-exist (admit tick + 1, when both are LEANING) does exercise iteration order through trajectory evaluation. Closes R1 RC-C C-3 (curve snapshot) + C-4 (iteration order) + R6 B7 (same-tick admission GIVEN) + R7 B11 (admit-tick observability).

### State Machine Transitions

- **AC-PW-11 (SPAWNED → LEANING)** — **GIVEN** valid params at `Construct()`, **WHEN** SPAWNED entry fires, **THEN** state → LEANING on next tick (one-tick SPAWNED hold) AND `target_lane`/`source_lane`/`lean_duration_s`/`forward_velocity_ms`/`LateralOffsetCurve` are frozen (late mutation attempts produce no observable change).
- **AC-PW-12 (LEANING → TRAVERSING; R2 Cluster A fix; R4 CF-A1 update; R5 B1 — `lean_duration_s` GIVEN updated 0.6 → 0.65 to match the post-R2-Cluster-E floor; R10d 2026-06-17 — `lean_duration_s` GIVEN updated 0.65 → 0.70 to match R10d FLOOR raise; values below the floor are floored at SPAWNED per Rule 5 so prior GIVENs were structurally impossible at runtime)** — **GIVEN** wave in LEANING with `lean_duration_s = 0.70` (R10d FLOOR), **WHEN** `lean_progress >= 1.0`, **THEN** state → TRAVERSING on that exact tick with no gap frame. `TraverseElapsedS` is initialized to `0.0` at TRAVERSING entry; the F-TRAJ-TNORM tick-order contract (read before increment) guarantees the first observable `t_norm` reading at or after TRAVERSING entry equals `0.0` (within `1e-5`) — reachable under accumulator semantics where it was unreachable under the prior wall-clock model. Test asserts the CF-A1 state-aware struct returns `{bWaveFound=true, State=TRAVERSING, ElapsedS=0.0}` AND derived `t_norm == 0.0` at the entry-tick observation point.
- **AC-PW-13 (TRAVERSING → LANDED via threshold-cross)** — **GIVEN** TRAVERSING wave with single-tick `DeltaTime` overshoot (`world_z` goes from +0.2 to −0.5), **WHEN** threshold-cross check fires, **THEN** state → LANDED on that same tick, `collision_outcome` set in that tick. (The `t_norm = 1.0 but world_z > 0.0` authoring-error sub-case is unreachable at runtime — cook-time tooling rejects velocity/offset mismatches via AC-PW-30; AC-PW-13 covers the threshold-cross runtime path only. R1 RC-E E-4 fix.)
- **AC-PW-14 (LANDED → DESPAWNING after hold)** — **GIVEN** wave in LANDED with `WAVE_DESPAWN_HOLD_S = 0.15`, **WHEN** 0.15s elapse (RUNNING+unpaused), **THEN** state → DESPAWNING at hold-expiry tick. No re-fires of OnWaveHit/OnNearMiss during hold.
- **AC-PW-15 (DESPAWNING pipeline ordering — R1 RC-E E-2 fix; R4 RC-R3-4 — Telegraph step independently observable via Seam 13 extension; R6 B1 — paper-only-seam-field naming corrections; R6 B4 — contiguity scoping strengthened against same-tick multi-despawn; R7 B4 — GIVEN extended with multi-wave-same-tick despawn scenario so the contiguity assertion is exercised against the bug class it was designed to catch)** — **GIVEN** a scenario satisfying **both** sub-conditions: (1) **Single-wave subcase**: natural-landing DESPAWNING entry for `wave_id = W` (covers basic ordering invariant); (2) **R7 B4 multi-wave subcase (REQUIRED — the contiguity assertion is vacuously satisfied without this)**: at least two waves entering DESPAWNING on the SAME tick — naturally produced by either (a) two waves with identical `target_lane` and identical velocity landing same tick (LANDED → DESPAWNING after `WAVE_DESPAWN_HOLD_S`), or (b) `FRSMTestStub.SetIsPaused(false)` from RUNNING(paused) with ≥3 LEANING/TRAVERSING waves active (pause-flush routes all to DESPAWNING in the same tick). **WHEN** the tick executes, **THEN** for EACH `wave_id` ∈ {despawning set in that tick}, within the same tick the calls fire in this exact order: (i) `IWaveSpawnerCallback::OnCollisionUnregistered(wave_id)` FIRST (Rule 13 step 1 — production class synchronously calls `Collision->UnregisterWave` in same tick); (ii) `IWaveSpawnerCallback::OnTelegraphUnregistered(wave_id)` SECOND (R4 Seam 13 extension — production class synchronously calls `Telegraph->UnregisterWave` in same tick — the single UnregisterWave call site per R6 B5); (iii) `IWaveSpawnerCallback::OnWaveDespawned(...)` THIRD. **Contiguity-scoped ordering assertion (R6 B4; R7 B4 — exercised against multi-wave subcase)**: the unified `FWaveSpawnerCallbackTestStub.GetEventLogInOrder()` event log MUST contain three entries for EACH `wave_id` in the despawning set as a **CONTIGUOUS RUN** — i.e., for every `wave_id`, the three entries for that wave_id appear at consecutive indices in the event log with NO entry for any other `wave_id` (or any other `EWaveEventType`) between them — in this exact sequence per wave_id: `[EWaveEventType::CollisionUnregistered(wave_id), EWaveEventType::TelegraphUnregistered(wave_id), EWaveEventType::Despawned(wave_id, despawn_reason, target_lane)]`. Across multiple despawning waves on the same tick, the inter-wave order MAY follow `wave_id ASC` (Rule 14(b) iteration invariant) but the CONTIGUITY of each wave's three entries is the binding assertion: a buggy "all step-(i) across all waves, then all step-(ii) across all waves" order produces a log where each wave's three entries are present and in correct relative order per-wave_id but interleaved with other wave_ids' entries — every per-wave contiguity check FAILS, surfacing the inter-wave interleave bug Rule 13's "atomic per wave" contract forbids. **Individual-counter assertions (R6 B1 — naming corrections to match seam doc)**: for each `wave_id` in the despawning set, `GetCollisionUnregisterCount` (cumulative) increments by 1 (no "ed" — the seam doc at `docs/architecture/platform-seam-interfaces.md` line 1640 declares `int32 GetCollisionUnregisterCount() const`; prior GDD spelling `GetCollisionUnregisteredCount` was paper-only and would fail compile) AND `GetTelegraphUnregisteredCount` increments by 1 AND `GetDespawnEventCount` increments by 1. **Enumerator naming (R6 B1)**: the event log entries use `EWaveEventType::Despawned` (NOT `WaveDespawned` — seam doc line 1658 declares `enum class EWaveEventType : uint8 { WaveHit, NearMiss, Despawned, CollisionUnregistered, TelegraphUnregistered }`; `WaveHit` and `NearMiss` carry the `Wave`/`Near` prefix but the despawn enumerator does NOT — prior GDD spelling `WaveDespawned` was paper-only and would fail compile). Steps 5–6 (state clear + pool return) MAY defer one frame.
- **AC-PW-16 (Pause-freeze + PauseFlush; R2 Cluster A fix; R4 RC-R3-3 — state-aware sentinel fix)** — **GIVEN** `FRSMTestStub.SetIsPaused(true)` while TRAVERSING (triggers `OnPausedChanged(true)` broadcast — Seam 7 R2 ext.), **WHEN** 20 ticks fire with **injected** `DeltaTime = 0.016` (tick harness must use injected delta — CF-A2), **THEN** five invariants assert (per R4 RC-R3-3, state assertion is now independent of accumulator assertion — closes the sentinel-ambiguity false positive): (a) `GetTraverseElapsedS_ForTest(WaveId).bWaveFound == true` (the wave is still in the active list — guards against a pause-flush bug routing the wave to DESPAWNING); (b) `GetTraverseElapsedS_ForTest(WaveId).State == TRAVERSING` (the wave is in the expected state — guards against wrong-state transitions during the freeze); (c) `GetTraverseElapsedS_ForTest(WaveId).ElapsedS` does not advance between tick 0 and tick 20 (within `1e-5`; underlying accumulator invariant — Rule 3 pause-freeze gate fires before any increment); (d) `t_norm` does not advance (derived invariant); (e) `world_z` does not decrease. All five MUST be asserted independently — passing (c) alone (the prior R3 form) is satisfiable by a bug that wrongly DESPAWNED the wave (the R3 sentinel returned `-1.0f` for both "not found" and "not TRAVERSING," letting the assertion pass with the wave gone). The state-aware struct now distinguishes those failures explicitly. On `SetIsPaused(false)` from RUNNING (broadcasts `OnPausedChanged(false)` — Seam 7 R2 ext.): wave → DESPAWNING with `despawn_reason = PauseFlush`. `FWaveSpawnerCallbackTestStub.GetLastDespawnReason() == PauseFlush` AND `GetWaveHitCount() == 0` AND `GetNearMissCount() == 0`. No hit events.
- **AC-PW-17 (EC-STALE-PAUSE-ABORTED — unfreeze + drain; R2 Cluster A fix)** — Two sub-cases covering both LEANING-state and TRAVERSING-state waves at the time of pause:
    - **AC-PW-17a (LEANING-state wave)**: **GIVEN** wave in LEANING with recorded `LeanProgress_pre_pause`, `FRSMTestStub.SetIsPaused(true)` (pauses for `P` ticks, injected `DeltaTime = 0.016` each — CF-A2), **WHEN** `FRSMTestStub.SetCurrentStateWithPauseClear(ABORTED)` is called (atomic ABORTED + is_paused→false on same tick; Seam 7 R2 ext.; `RSM.OnPausedChanged` does NOT fire), **THEN** wave does NOT route to DESPAWNING via PauseFlush; `LeanProgress` resumes from `LeanProgress_pre_pause` (not from 0, not from a wall-clock-derived position); wave advances LEANING → TRAVERSING → LANDED → DESPAWNING with `despawn_reason = RunTermination`. `FWaveSpawnerCallbackTestStub.GetLastDespawnReason() == RunTermination`.
    - **AC-PW-17b (TRAVERSING-state wave — no-teleport invariant; R4 RC-R3-3 state-aware sentinel fix; R7 B12 — N upper bound added so `1e-4` tolerance stays valid)**: **GIVEN** wave in TRAVERSING with recorded pre-pause query `Q_pre = GetTraverseElapsedS_ForTest(WaveId)` (CF-A1 R4 state-aware struct: `Q_pre.bWaveFound == true && Q_pre.State == TRAVERSING && Q_pre.ElapsedS == TraverseElapsedS_pre_pause`), paused for `P` ticks (injected delta — CF-A2), AND `N_MAX = 391` (the post-unfreeze tick cap below which the `1e-4` arithmetic tolerance is structurally sound — see derivation note below). **WHEN** `FRSMTestStub.SetCurrentStateWithPauseClear(ABORTED)` fires (Seam 7 R2 ext.; `OnPausedChanged` does NOT fire), **THEN** wave does NOT PauseFlush; the wave remains in TRAVERSING through unfreeze; after `N` post-unfreeze ticks each with injected `DeltaTime = 0.016` where **`N ≤ N_MAX = 391`** (BINDING upper bound — at 60fps this corresponds to `391 × 0.016 = 6.256s` of post-unfreeze advancement, fully covering the maximum `TravelDurationS = 6.25s` at min velocity 4.0 m/s + max offset 25.0m per F-TRAVERSE-DURATION (R8 sys-d B-4 — N_MAX bumped from R7's 375 which yielded only 6.0s coverage and failed the "matching max TravelDurationS" rationale by 0.25s); the test harness MUST NOT run with N > 391 because IEEE 754 single-precision rounding error in `Σ DeltaTime` exceeds the `1e-4` tolerance above this bound), the post-unfreeze query `Q_post = GetTraverseElapsedS_ForTest(WaveId)` satisfies **three independent assertions**: (i) `Q_post.bWaveFound == true` (wave not flushed); (ii) `Q_post.State == TRAVERSING` (wave still progressing — guards against wrong-state transitions during the abort path); (iii) `|Q_post.ElapsedS − (Q_pre.ElapsedS + N × 0.016)| ≤ 1e-4` (arithmetic no-teleport — pause duration NOT included in the accumulator). **N_MAX derivation (R7 B12)**: the `1e-4` tolerance accommodates IEEE 754 single-precision rounding error in `Σ DeltaTime` over typical accumulator values (TraverseElapsedS is declared `float` in FPullWaveInstanceState — single-precision epsilon at 1–4s values is ~1e-7, so per-tick rounding contributes ~1e-7 worst-case). After N ticks the accumulated worst-case error scales as `N × 1e-7 × C` where C accounts for non-uniform tick contributions to the mantissa; at N=391 the bound is ~9.4e-5 (just under `1e-4`); at N=1000 the bound is ~2.4e-4 (above `1e-4` — a correct implementation produces a spurious failure). N_MAX=391 is the safe ceiling at 60fps (R8 sys-d B-4 — bumped from R7's 375 to fully cover max TravelDurationS=6.25s); harnesses requiring longer post-unfreeze advancement (e.g., soak-test paths) MUST partition into multiple AC-PW-17b runs each with N ≤ 391 and Q_pre re-captured between runs. A teleport bug would produce a difference equal to the pause duration `P × 0.016` (≥ tens of milliseconds = ≥ 0.01), comfortably above the `1e-4` bar at any valid N. Critically, (i)+(ii) are independently asserted from (iii) — the R3 sentinel form `-1.0f` would have passed (iii)-equivalent on either a found-and-TRAVERSING wave OR a flushed wave; the state-aware struct now distinguishes those failures. `world_z` advances smoothly from its pre-pause value with no positional teleport. Wave drains TRAVERSING → LANDED → DESPAWNING with `despawn_reason = RunTermination`. LANDED hit/near-miss events fire if conditions met, observable via `FWaveSpawnerCallbackTestStub.GetWaveHitCount()` / `GetNearMissCount()` (Seam 13 R2 ext.). Cross-reference: AC-PW-26 for the distinguishability assertion at a higher level.
    - Both sub-cases distinguish EC-STALE-PAUSE-ABORTED from EC-PAUSE-FLUSH. The no-teleport arithmetic assertion in (17b) is the primary regression guard for Cluster A: it is violated if and only if wall-clock time is reintroduced into trajectory evaluation during resume.
- **AC-PW-18 (Run-termination drain — no RSM flip)** — **GIVEN** `FRSMTestStub.SetCurrentState(DEAD)` during TRAVERSING, **WHEN** wave continues through TRAVERSING → LANDED, **THEN** `OnWaveHit` fires at LANDED if conditions met AND `despawn_reason = RunTermination`. Pull-Wave does NOT call RSM (state remains DEAD throughout).

- **AC-PW-MID-TICK-PAUSE-DEFERRAL (R7 B13 — NEW R7; R8 RC-A 2026-06-11 — GIVEN rewritten to use only declared Seam 7 + Seam 13 operations; closes paper-only-seam-OP gap on the "fire on iteration index N" hook that was never authored in FRSMTestStub; RC-G concurrency / lifecycle race binding; closes mid-tick `OnPausedChanged` delegate fire ambiguity; R10c — Pull-Wave R9 qa-lead F1 2026-06-17 — re-entrant user callback now binds via `FWaveSpawnerCallbackTestStub.SetOnDespawnedUserCallback(...)` (Seam 13 R10c ext.); closes paper-only-seam-FIELD gap on the OnWaveDespawned re-entrant listener slot that the R8 RC-A rewrite had cited as "binds an OnWaveDespawned listener" without a declared slot to bind to)** — **Gate**: BLOCKING. **GIVEN** the following scenario, constructed using only declared seam operations on `FRSMTestStub` (Seam 7: `SetCurrentState`, `SetRemainingTime`, `SetIsPaused`, `SetCurrentStateWithPauseClear`, `OnPausedChanged` multicast delegate) and `FWaveSpawnerCallbackTestStub` (Seam 13: `OnWaveDespawned` listener method + `SetOnDespawnedUserCallback(TFunction<void(int32, EWaveDespawnReason, int32)>)` re-entrant user callback slot fired from inside `OnWaveDespawned` AFTER standard recording — R10c ext.): three waves authored such that wave_id 1 is in LANDED state at tick T start with `LandedHoldElapsedS` exactly at `WAVE_DESPAWN_HOLD_S` (about to transition LANDED → DESPAWNING this tick — naturally produced by spawning wave_id 1 in TRAVERSING at tick T0 with `TravelDurationS` matching the `T − T0 − WAVE_DESPAWN_HOLD_S` interval); wave_id 2 in TRAVERSING (mid-flight); wave_id 3 in LEANING (mid-lean). Test setup registers a re-entrant user callback via `FWaveSpawnerCallbackTestStub.SetOnDespawnedUserCallback(...)` (Seam 13 R10c ext.) that, on receiving the despawn event for wave_id 1 with `despawn_reason == NaturalLanding`, **synchronously calls `FRSMTestStub.SetIsPaused(false)` from RUNNING(paused=true)** — which fires `OnPausedChanged(false)` (Seam 7 R2 ext.). The user-callback slot fires from inside `OnWaveDespawned` AFTER all standard event recording (event log push, counters, `bCollisionUnregisteredBeforeBroadcast` capture) is complete for wave_id 1, so re-entrant `OnPausedChanged(false)` does NOT corrupt wave_id 1's recording invariants. Tick T begins with `FRSMTestStub.SetIsPaused(true)` already set at tick T-1 boundary (so RSM is paused at tick T start, but the GIVEN's pre-pause states freeze the waves at their pre-pause progress per Rule 3 freeze-gate; wave_id 1's LANDED hold completes via injected `DeltaTime` reaching `WAVE_DESPAWN_HOLD_S` at tick T-1 boundary, scheduling the natural despawn at tick T). **Mechanism**: the OnWaveDespawned-handler-triggered SetIsPaused fires `OnPausedChanged(false)` AFTER wave_id 1's despawn pipeline completes (step (iii) `Despawned` event triggers the listener) but BEFORE wave_id 2 and wave_id 3 are iterated in the per-wave tick loop. This is the mid-tick fire scenario, constructed entirely from declared seam operations without any "fire on iteration index N" hook injection. **WHEN** Pull-Wave's per-wave iteration completes for waves 1, 2, 3 in the current tick T AND the next tick T+1 begins, **THEN** five invariants assert: (i) inside tick T (the tick during which `OnPausedChanged(false)` fired mid-iteration via the OnWaveDespawned-handler chain), wave_id 1 despawns NORMALLY with `despawn_reason == NaturalLanding` (NOT PauseFlush); waves 2 and 3 do NOT transition to DESPAWNING with `despawn_reason = PauseFlush` during tick T — `FWaveSpawnerCallbackTestStub.GetEventLogInOrder()` for tick T records the natural despawn of wave_id 1 only, no PauseFlush entries (the delegate handler only set the `bPauseFlushPending` flag); (ii) tick T's per-wave tick bodies for waves 2 and 3 execute normally per their respective state's tick contract — wave_id 2's TraverseElapsedS advances by injected DeltaTime, wave_id 3's LeanProgress advances normally (the OnPausedChanged(false) fire un-paused RSM, so post-OnPausedChanged ticks proceed un-frozen for waves 2 and 3); (iii) at the START of tick T+1, BEFORE the per-wave iteration loop begins, the deferred batch flush executes: waves 2 and 3 each route to DESPAWNING with `despawn_reason = PauseFlush` in `wave_id ASC` order (wave_id 1 already despawned at tick T NaturalLanding and is not in the flush batch); (iv) `GetEventLogInOrder()` across tick T + tick T+1 records the 3 waves' DESPAWNING pipelines as 3 CONTIGUOUS RUNS — wave_id 1's at tick T `[CollisionUnregistered(1), TelegraphUnregistered(1), Despawned(1, NaturalLanding)]` (NaturalLanding, not PauseFlush); waves 2 and 3 at tick T+1 `[CollisionUnregistered(2), TelegraphUnregistered(2), Despawned(2, PauseFlush), CollisionUnregistered(3), TelegraphUnregistered(3), Despawned(3, PauseFlush)]` in wave_id ASC order — verifying AC-PW-15 R7 B4 contiguity assertion holds at the multi-wave flush boundary; (v) `GetWaveHitCount() == 0` (no hit on wave_id 1 NaturalLanding because the player condition is set such that wave_id 1's `target_lane != PM stub current_lane`) AND `GetNearMissCount() == 0` (no LANDED hit/near-miss events fire during the flush). The 5 assertions together exercise the queued-to-next-tick semantic AND verify it integrates correctly with Rule 13's atomic-per-wave despawn pipeline AND verify the mid-tick fire path works correctly when triggered by a despawn-event-chain rather than by a "fire on iteration index N" hook. **Failure modes detected**: an inline mid-tick flush implementation would route waves 2, 3 to DESPAWNING during tick T (mid-iteration), violating assertion (i); a flush that fires immediately at next-tick top but before atomicity-per-wave is established would interleave the 2 flushed waves' step-(i)/step-(ii)/step-(iii) calls, violating assertion (iv); an OnPausedChanged handler that fires re-entrantly into Pull-Wave's tick (mutating state mid-iteration) would corrupt wave_id 2's TraverseElapsedS advancement, violating assertion (ii). **R8 RC-A rewrite rationale**: the prior R7 GIVEN cited Seam 7's "fire on iteration index N" injection hook on `FRSMTestStub`, but `FRSMTestStub` declares only the operations enumerated above — no iteration-index hook exists. The Seam 13 OnWaveDespawned listener path uses ONLY declared seam operations on Seam 7 (no new ops) and naturally produces a mid-tick OnPausedChanged fire AFTER wave_id 1's despawn but BEFORE wave_id 2's tick body. **R10c amendment (2026-06-17)**: R9 qa-lead F1 surfaced that the R8 rewrite phrased "binds an OnWaveDespawned listener on `FWaveSpawnerCallbackTestStub`" but the stub at R8-time had no declared slot to bind a re-entrant user callback to — a paper-only-seam-FIELD gap (the inverse of the paper-only-seam-OP gap R8 RC-A closed). R10c extends Seam 13's stub with the `SetOnDespawnedUserCallback(TFunction<...>)` slot + fires it from inside `OnWaveDespawned` after standard recording; production class (`FWaveSpawnerCallback_Production`) is unchanged — production has no use for a re-entrant user callback, so the slot is correctly stub-only. Seam-doc footprint: ~30 lines added to `FWaveSpawnerCallbackTestStub` body (slot member, setter, has-check, invocation, Reset clear) + 1 row added to "GDD ACs Unblocked by This Stub" table. No new IWaveSpawnerCallback interface methods.

### Core Rule Behaviors

- **AC-PW-TIER0-LEANING (R4 RC-R3-2 — NEW; R5 B1 — `LeanDurationS` GIVEN updated 0.6 → 0.65 to match floor; R10d 2026-06-17 — `LeanDurationS` GIVEN updated 0.65 → 0.70 to match R10d FLOOR raise; values below the floor are floored at SPAWNED per Rule 5)** — **GIVEN** wave spawned with `SourceLane == TargetLane == 2` (tier-0 straight-forward; pre-computed `lean_magnitude_tier = 0` at SPAWNED entry), `LeanDurationS = 0.70` (R10d FLOOR), **WHEN** Pull-Wave is ticked across the full LEANING duration via Seam 7 / CF-A2 injected delta, **THEN** the exposed Data Contract fields each tick satisfy: (a) `lean_magnitude_tier == 0`; (b) `lean_direction_sign == 0`; (c) `lean_angle_deg == 0.0°` throughout LEANING (within `1e-5`; LEAN_ANGLE_TIER0_DEG = 0.0 locked); (d) `LeanChargeIntensity_scalar` ramps `1.0 → LEAN_BRIGHTNESS_PEAK_RATIO` via the eased curve, with `LeanChargeIntensity_scalar(lean_progress = 0.4) ≈ 1.0 + 0.15 × (LEAN_BRIGHTNESS_PEAK_RATIO − 1.0)` and `LeanChargeIntensity_scalar(lean_progress = 1.0) == LEAN_BRIGHTNESS_PEAK_RATIO` (within `1e-5`); (e) wave transitions LEANING → TRAVERSING normally at `lean_progress >= 1.0`. Verifies tier-0 is NOT special-cased as visually mute — brightness ramp is identical to tiers 1–4 (Channel-separation rationale). Closes the R3 RC-R3-2 "tier-0 undefined" gap.
- **AC-PW-19 (Rule 4 target_lane immutability)** — **GIVEN** wave at SPAWNED with `target_lane = 3`, **WHEN** any external caller attempts mutation, **THEN** `target_lane` remains `3` for full lifecycle. Other params similarly asserted unchanged.
- **AC-PW-20 (Rule 6 spawn-time velocity binding — DPC R7 forward contract; R5 B1 — epsilon `1e-9` → `1e-5` per IEEE 754 single-precision floor)** — **GIVEN** wave spawns during OPENER with `ForwardVelocityMs = 5.0`, RSM advances through MID into PEAK during TRAVERSING, **WHEN** `world_z` sampled across 10 ticks, **THEN** wave's `forward_velocity_ms` remains `5.0` on every tick; `world_z` computed against `5.0` matches observed within `1e-5`. Closes OQ-DPC-2 as NO phase-driven velocity scaling.

### Hit / Near-Miss Resolution

- **AC-PW-21a (hit — SETTLED on target lane)** — **GIVEN** wave with `target_lane = 2` reaches LANDED, `FPlayerMovementTestStub` SETTLED + `current_lane = 2`, **WHEN** LANDED entry, **THEN** `OnWaveHit(wave_id, target_lane=2, ...)` published; `OnNearMiss` NOT published. **Observed via:** `FWaveSpawnerCallbackTestStub.GetWaveHitCount() == 1` AND `GetNearMissCount() == 0` (Seam 13 R2 ext. — bind stub to `PullWave->OnWaveHit` + `OnNearMiss` delegates in test setup).
- **AC-PW-21b (hit — SLIPPING toward target lane)** — **GIVEN** wave `target_lane = 2`, stub SLIPPING + `target_lane = 2`, **WHEN** LANDED entry, **THEN** `OnWaveHit` published; `OnNearMiss` NOT. **Observed via:** `FWaveSpawnerCallbackTestStub.GetWaveHitCount() == 1` AND `GetNearMissCount() == 0` (Seam 13 R2 ext.).
- **AC-PW-21c (clean miss)** — **GIVEN** wave `target_lane = 2`, stub SETTLED + `current_lane = 4`, **WHEN** LANDED entry, **THEN** neither event fires. `collision_outcome == CleanMiss`. **Observed via:** `GetWaveHitCount() == 0` AND `GetNearMissCount() == 0` (Seam 13 R2 ext.).
- **AC-PW-22 (near-miss — direct read of `current_lane` during SLIPPING, R1 RC-A fix)** — **GIVEN** wave `target_lane = 3` in TRAVERSING reaches LANDED, `FPlayerMovementTestStub.SetMovementState(SLIPPING)` AND `FPlayerMovementTestStub.SetCurrentLane(3)` AND `FPlayerMovementTestStub.SetTargetLane(4)` (player is mid-slip from lane 3 → lane 4 — `current_lane` returns SOURCE per PM Rules 4 + 7), **WHEN** LANDED entry evaluates Rule 11, **THEN** `OnNearMiss(wave_id, target_lane=3, slipped_from_lane=3)` published AND `FPlayerMovementTestStub.GetNearMissBeatCount() == 1`. `OnWaveHit` NOT published. No subscription to `OnSlipMidpoint` is required by Pull-Wave; `FireSlipMidpoint(...)` is NOT invoked in this test path.
- **AC-PW-23 (EC-NEARMISS-PREMIDPOINT — DELETED, R1 RC-A)** — *AC ID retained as DELETED for historical traceability.* The R1-draft pre-midpoint fallback inference is superseded by direct `current_lane` read; the EC it tested no longer exists (PM Rules 4 + 7 guarantee `current_lane` is correct from SLIPPING entry, before any midpoint). No test coverage required; no implementation surface remains. See Rule 11 and EC-NEARMISS-PREMIDPOINT (REMOVED).

### Rule 12 — Independence

- **AC-PW-24 (no cross-wave physics)** — **GIVEN** 3 waves in TRAVERSING with different params, **WHEN** ticked simultaneously 50 frames, **THEN** each wave's `(world_x, world_z, t_norm)` matches its own param computation (zero inter-wave coupling). Two waves at same voxel produce no trajectory modification.

### Rule 13 — Forced Despawn

- **AC-PW-25 (forced despawn — no hit/near-miss event, EC-FORCED-DESPAWN-NO-EVENT)** — **GIVEN** TRAVERSING wave forced to DESPAWNING by PauseFlush (driven by `FRSMTestStub.SetIsPaused(false)` from RUNNING(paused) — Seam 7 R2 ext., `OnPausedChanged(false)` fires), **WHEN** DESPAWNING completes, **THEN** `FWaveSpawnerCallbackTestStub.GetLastDespawnReason() == PauseFlush` AND `GetWaveHitCount() == 0` AND `GetNearMissCount() == 0` (Seam 13 R2 ext.). Same assertion applies for RunTermination via ABORTED path (driven by `SetCurrentStateWithPauseClear(ABORTED)` — Seam 7 R2 ext.): `GetLastDespawnReason() == RunTermination` AND hit/near-miss counts remain 0.

### Edge Case Coverage

- **AC-PW-26 (EC-PAUSE-FLUSH vs EC-STALE-PAUSE-ABORTED distinguishability)** — Two test instances: (A) RUNNING(paused=true) → RUNNING(paused=false) = PauseFlush; (B) RUNNING(paused=false) → ABORTED = RunTermination. (A): all waves get PauseFlush, no hit events. (B): waves drain TRAVERSING → LANDED → DESPAWNING with RunTermination, LANDED hits fire if conditions met. Both instances require `FRSMTestStub.OnPausedChanged` (Seam 7 R2 ext.) and `FWaveSpawnerCallbackTestStub.GetEventLogInOrder()` (Seam 13 R2 ext.) for observation. The arithmetic no-teleport assertion for case (B)'s TRAVERSING-state waves is specified in full in AC-PW-17b — see that AC for the `TraverseElapsedS` tolerance and formula.
- **AC-PW-27 (EC-DPC-INACTIVE-AT-SPAWN — spawn rejected)** — **Build config**: Shipping or Test. **GIVEN** `DPC.GetCurrentFrameState().is_active == false` at `Construct()`, **WHEN** SPAWNED-entry sanity check, **THEN** wave rejected (no SPAWNED entry, no LEANING reached), Wave Spawner slot restored, `FTestLogCapture` records `LogPullWave, Warning`. Process remains alive.
- **AC-PW-28 (EC-CONCURRENT-LANDING-SAME-LANE)** — Two waves with identical `target_lane = 2`, identical velocities, same-tick LANDED, stub SETTLED + `current_lane = 2`. Both independently publish `OnWaveHit` (two separate events, same tick, distinct `wave_id`). No deduplication. **Observed via:** `FWaveSpawnerCallbackTestStub.GetWaveHitCount() == 2` AND `GetEventLogInOrder()` contains two `EWaveEventType::WaveHit` entries in `WaveId ASCENDING` order (Seam 13 R2 ext. — AC-PW-10 ordering invariant).
- **AC-PW-29 (EC-WAVE-LIFETIME-OVERRUN — cook-time warning)** — **Gate**: ADVISORY (cook-time tooling). Pattern with `ForwardVelocityMs = 4.0` + `SPAWN_PLANE_Z_OFFSET_M = 26.0`. Tooling logs `LogPullWaveTooling, Warning` referencing `TravelDurationS > 5.0s`; pattern NOT rejected (warning, not error).
- **AC-PW-30 (EC-CURVE-NULL/OVERSHOOT/ENDPOINT-DRIFT/ZERO-VELOCITY — cook-time rejection)** — **Build config**: Shipping or Test. Four independent test patterns: (i) `LateralOffsetCurve == nullptr`; (ii) `GetFloatValue(0.5) = 1.5`; (iii) `GetFloatValue(1.0) = 1.1` (drift > tolerance); (iv) `ForwardVelocityMs = 0.0`. All four rejected at cook time. `FTestLogCapture.CountMessages(LogPullWave, ELogVerbosity::Error) == 4`. No runtime state created.
- **AC-PW-31 (EC-LANDING-OVERSHOOT-FORWARD — threshold-cross handles frame-hitch)** — TRAVERSING wave at `world_z = +0.3` tick N; simulated hitch jumps `world_z` to `−0.8` tick N+1. **THEN** LANDED triggers tick N+1 (not deferred), `collision_outcome` evaluated at tick-N+1 position (NOT interpolated back to `world_z = 0`).
- **AC-PW-32 (EC-INTER-SLIP-GATE-INTRODUCTION — invariant alert; R8 RC-B inversion 2026-06-11 — Option-(c) MIN_ESCAPE_SLIPS=2 reduces inter-slip gate demand; R10c — Pull-Wave R9 qa-lead F6 2026-06-17 — multi-frame gate math rebuilt with explicit K-frame formula and per-fps frame_dt; R10d — FLOOR raised 0.65 → 0.70s 2026-06-17, K-frame table re-derived with widened margins)** — **Gate**: ADVISORY (static analysis at PM revision review). Verifies the **K-frame inter-slip gate budget formula** `REACTION_BUDGET + SLIP_TWEEN × MIN_ESCAPE_SLIPS + MIN_ESCAPE_SLIPS × K × frame_dt ≤ TELEGRAPH_WINDOW_FLOOR_S` after any PM inter-slip gate introduction, where `K` is the inter-slip gate frames-per-slip (K=1 is the baseline single-frame gate; K≥2 are multi-frame gates) and `frame_dt` is the per-frame interval at the target frame rate (60fps → 0.0167s; 30fps → 0.0333s). Under R10d defaults (`SLIP_TWEEN = 0.15s`, `MIN_ESCAPE_SLIPS = 2`, `REACT = 0.20s`, `FLOOR = 0.70s`), the K=1 baseline passes with **200ms margin** at default REACT and **150ms margin** at REACT=0.25 ceiling. **K=1 baseline survives across the full REACT safe range and 30/60fps Hardware Contract envelope with widened margins under R10d**: 60fps at REACT=0.20 `0.20 + 0.30 + 2 × 1 × 0.0167 = 0.533s ≤ 0.70s` ✓ **167ms margin (≈10 frames)**; 30fps at REACT=0.20 `0.20 + 0.30 + 2 × 1 × 0.0333 = 0.567s ≤ 0.70s` ✓ **133ms margin (≈4 frames at 30Hz)**; 60fps at REACT=0.25 ceiling `0.25 + 0.30 + 2 × 1 × 0.0167 = 0.583s ≤ 0.70s` ✓ **117ms margin (≈7 frames)**; 30fps at REACT=0.25 ceiling `0.25 + 0.30 + 2 × 1 × 0.0333 = 0.617s ≤ 0.70s` ✓ **83ms margin (≈2 frames at 30Hz)**. The pre-Option-(c) conclusion "any inter-slip gate at any frame rate immediately violates" is **inverted** by the 2026-06-11 cross-system coordination + R10d FLOOR raise — at MIN_ESCAPE_SLIPS=2 + FLOOR=0.70s the K=1 budget absorbs one inter-slip frame at both 60fps and 30fps with ≥2 frames of margin across the full REACT safe range. **R10d binding ceiling case**: 30fps + REACT=0.25 has 83ms margin (was 33ms / 1-frame under pre-R10d FLOOR=0.65s); the practical-zero K=1 binding case is retired under R10d. **Practical warning**: the R10d FLOOR raise also extends mathematical viability into multi-frame gates (see table below); the new binding constraint is K=2 at 30fps (17ms margin = ~0.5 frame at 30Hz, effectively zero practical margin under frame-pacer jitter, vsync stalls, or platform-level deferred frames). PM authors introducing ANY multi-frame inter-slip gate must verify across the full Hardware Contract's frame-rate range, NOT just 60fps default. **Reversion triggers**: if AC-PW-PEAK-NO-ADJACENT-CLUSTER is relaxed (re-admits consecutive triplets) MIN_ESCAPE_SLIPS reverts to 3 and the pre-Option-(c) cliff returns; if DPC FLOOR is reverted below 0.70s, R10d's safety margin collapses — flagged to PM and DPC as a cross-system constraint review trigger on any such change. **Multi-frame inter-slip gates (R10d K-frame table at FLOOR=0.70s)** — applying the K-frame formula `REACT + SLIP_TWEEN × M + M × K × frame_dt` (M = MIN_ESCAPE_SLIPS = 2, frame_dt per fps as above) under REACT=0.25 ceiling and FLOOR=0.70s:

| K | 60fps (`frame_dt = 0.0167s`) — `0.25 + 0.30 + 2×K×0.0167` | 30fps (`frame_dt = 0.0333s`) — `0.25 + 0.30 + 2×K×0.0333` |
|---|---|---|
| 1 (baseline) | 0.583s ✓ **117ms margin (≈7 frames)** | 0.617s ✓ **83ms margin (≈2-3 frames)** |
| 2 | 0.617s ✓ **83ms margin (≈5 frames)** | 0.683s ✓ **17ms margin (≈0.5 frame — effectively zero practical margin under jitter)** |
| 3 | 0.650s ✓ **50ms margin (≈3 frames)** | 0.750s ✗ **FAILS by 50ms (≈1.5 frames over FLOOR)** |
| 4 | 0.683s ✓ **17ms margin (≈1 frame — effectively zero practical margin under jitter)** | 0.817s ✗ **FAILS by 117ms (≈3.5 frames over FLOOR)** |
| ≥5 | 0.717s+ ✗ **FAILS by ≥17ms at 60fps** | 0.883s+ ✗ **FAILS by ≥183ms at 30fps** |

The R9 qa-lead F6 finding (30fps vs 60fps frame_dt confusion in the pre-R10c multi-frame paragraph) was closed at R10c by the explicit K-frame formula AND the per-fps frame_dt declaration. R10d re-derives the table cells against FLOOR=0.70s; each cell's arithmetic is grounded in its row's K and its column's `frame_dt`. **R10d practical binding**: 30fps now survives K=1 with measurable margin (≈2-3 frames) and K=2 at practical-zero (~0.5 frame margin); K≥3 fails at 30fps under REACT=0.25 ceiling. 60fps now survives K=1, K=2, K=3 all with measurable margin, sits at practical-zero margin at K=4, fails at K≥5. PM authors MUST verify any introduced multi-frame inter-slip gate against BOTH the 60fps AND 30fps columns at the REACT=0.25 ceiling, not just the 60fps default; the 30fps K=2 17ms cell is the new binding practical-zero case PM authors must NOT treat as "passes" without frame-pacer-jitter empirical validation. Requires PM revision review cross-sign-off from Pull-Wave author before introducing any multi-frame inter-slip gate.

### R7 Forward Contract Closure

- **AC-PW-PILLAR-2-BARRAGE-SPATIAL-K (R4 RC-R3-5 — strengthened to pattern-class diversity per R3 CD adjudication #5; R5 B4 — gate-level unified with sibling Wave-Spawner-dependent ACs; R7 B8 — K_class floor revised ≥6 → ≥4 because classes (1) and (2) were retired by AC-PW-PEAK-NO-ADJACENT-CLUSTER for REACTION_BUDGET / Hick's Law survivability reasons; R8 RC-C 2026-06-11 — min_patterns_per_class ≥ 2 floor added + class-3 B11 relaxation exception)** — **Gate**: BLOCKING at story-Done once Wave Spawner GDD + pattern pool are authored; until then ADVISORY (forward contract on a not-yet-written downstream GDD; same precondition class as AC-PW-WAVE-SPAWNER-PEAK-VELOCITY-FLOOR). **Classification**: Static analysis (cook-time). **GIVEN** Wave Spawner PEAK pool's `is_barrage=true` patterns; `NUM_LANES = 5`; `MIN_BARRAGE_LANE_SEPARATION = 1` (target-lane distinct — R2 Cluster E CD adjudication); M=3 occupies 3 distinct **target**-lane indices; AND the adjacent-cluster exclusion from AC-PW-PEAK-NO-ADJACENT-CLUSTER is enforced upstream (the three triplets `{0,1,2}` / `{1,2,3}` / `{2,3,4}` are excluded from PEAK admission); AND the R8 RC-C class-3 B11 relaxation contract permits mixed-tier barrages on K_class == 3 only (triplet `{0,2,4}`). **WHEN** cook-time tooling enumerates the admissible (post-exclusion) pool and classifies each M=3 triplet into mechanically-distinct **escape-strategy classes** (NOT raw triplet count). The taxonomy (R7 B8 — classes (1) and (2) retired): ~~(1) `consecutive-cluster-flee-opposite-edge` — retired by AC-PW-PEAK-NO-ADJACENT-CLUSTER R7 B8 (`{0,1,2}` and `{2,3,4}` excluded)~~; ~~(2) `center-cluster-flee-any-edge` — retired by AC-PW-PEAK-NO-ADJACENT-CLUSTER R7 B8 (`{1,2,3}` excluded)~~; (3) `even-split-middle-commit` — `{0,2,4}` forces lane 1 or 3; (4) `outer-pair-plus-inner-middle-commit` — `{0,1,4}` or `{0,3,4}` forces middle escape; (5) `asymmetric-pair-plus-far-flee-asymmetric` — `{0,2,3}` or `{1,2,4}`; (6) `inner-pair-plus-far-flee-bilateral` — `{0,1,3}` or `{1,3,4}` permits either-side flee. Each class encodes a distinct slip-chain decision (escape direction count + escape lane location). Admissible classes post-R7-B8: classes 3, 4, 5, 6 — exactly four. **THEN** five sub-checks pass: (i) **K_class coverage**: the PEAK pool covers all 4 of these post-exclusion classes (K_class ≥ 4, R7 B8 — reduced from R4's ≥6 because classes (1) and (2) are retired by AC-PW-PEAK-NO-ADJACENT-CLUSTER). (ii) **K_raw enumeration**: the raw post-exclusion count K_raw = 10 − 3 = **7** ≥ 4 (Pillar 2 spatial diversity floor — 7/4 = 1.75× margin per R9 coordination 2026-06-11). (iii) **Min-patterns-per-class floor (R8 RC-C — NEW 2026-06-11)**: each admissible K_class contains at least `MIN_PATTERNS_PER_K_CLASS = 2` distinct patterns. Class-4 (`{0,1,4}, {0,3,4}`), class-5 (`{0,2,3}, {1,2,4}`), class-6 (`{0,1,3}, {1,3,4}`) each contain ≥2 raw triplets and satisfy the floor naturally; class-3 (sole triplet `{0,2,4}`) satisfies the floor via the R8 RC-C B11 relaxation exception (mixed-tier barrages on class-3 admit ≥2 distinct configurations). (iv) **Class-3 mixed-tier admission (R8 RC-C — NEW 2026-06-11)**: mixed-tier patterns are admitted exclusively for triplet `{0,2,4}`; cook-time tooling rejects mixed-tier configurations for any other triplet (preserving R7 B11 uniform-tier as BLOCKING outside class-3). (v) **R9 cross-system survivability binding (NEW 2026-06-11)**: the surviving 7 triplets are the F-BARRAGE-SURVIVABILITY-INVARIANT MIN_ESCAPE_SLIPS = 2 worst-case-escape enumeration domain; if NUM_LANES or MIN_BARRAGE_LANE_SEPARATION changes, MIN_ESCAPE_SLIPS must be re-derived against the new surviving-triplet × player-lane enumeration. Tooling log includes `SPATIAL_K_CLASS: K_class=N ≥ 4 ✓` AND `SPATIAL_K_RAW: K_raw=7 ≥ 4 (1.75× margin) ✓` AND `MIN_PATTERNS_PER_CLASS: min=M ≥ 2 ✓` AND `CLASS_3_MIXED_TIER_ADMISSION: count=K admitted exclusively for triplet {0,2,4} ✓` AND `CROSS_SYS_SURVIVABILITY: MIN_ESCAPE_SLIPS=2 verified across 7 × 5 = 35 (triplet, player-lane) combinations ✓`. CI grep succeeds. Strengthening rationale: K_raw admits trivially-similar patterns (e.g., `{0,1,3}` and `{0,1,4}` both flee right but exercise different slip-chain lengths); pattern-class diversity ensures the PEAK pool actually rehearses 4 distinct escape decisions in the player's instinct, not 4 nearly-identical reflexes. The R8 RC-C min_patterns_per_class floor closes the K_class-floor-at-ceiling vacuous-satisfaction gap (K_class=4 = K_class_ceiling=4 admits singletons per class). Any reduction of `NUM_LANES` below 5 OR raise of `MIN_BARRAGE_LANE_SEPARATION` above 1 OR relaxation of AC-PW-PEAK-NO-ADJACENT-CLUSTER must re-run tooling, re-derive MIN_ESCAPE_SLIPS, AND update all five K metrics before story-Done.

- **AC-PW-BARRAGE-UNIFORM-VELOCITY (R4 RC-R3-5 — NEW; closes OQ-PW-2; R5 B4 — gate-level unified with sibling Wave-Spawner-dependent ACs)** — **Gate**: BLOCKING at story-Done once Wave Spawner GDD + pattern pool are authored; until then ADVISORY (forward contract on a not-yet-written downstream GDD; same precondition class as AC-PW-WAVE-SPAWNER-PEAK-VELOCITY-FLOOR). **Classification**: Static cross-pattern consistency check (cook-time). **GIVEN** any Wave Spawner barrage pattern (members emitted within `BARRAGE_SIMULTANEITY_WINDOW_S`), **WHEN** cook-time tooling reads each member's authored `ForwardVelocityMs`, **THEN** all members share identical `ForwardVelocityMs` (literal `float` equality; no tolerance). Tooling log includes `BARRAGE_VEL_UNIFORM: pattern=NAME members_velocity=X.X m/s ✓`. Failure halts admission; pattern rejected with error referencing F-BARRAGE-SURVIVABILITY-INVARIANT (mixed velocity invalidates the uniform-timing escape budget). Distinct barrage patterns within the same phase pool MAY use different uniform velocities — the constraint is within-barrage only.

- **AC-PW-OPENER-NO-BARRAGE (R4 RC-R3-5 — NEW; R5 B4 — gate-level unified with sibling Wave-Spawner-dependent ACs)** — **Gate**: BLOCKING at story-Done once Wave Spawner GDD + pattern pool are authored; until then ADVISORY (forward contract on a not-yet-written downstream GDD; same precondition class as AC-PW-WAVE-SPAWNER-PEAK-VELOCITY-FLOOR). **Classification**: Static cook-time check. **GIVEN** Wave Spawner OPENER pattern pool, **WHEN** cook-time tooling reads each pattern's `is_barrage` field, **THEN** no OPENER-pool pattern has `is_barrage = true`. Tooling log includes `OPENER_NO_BARRAGE: opener_pool_size=N barrage_count=0 ✓`. Failure halts admission with error referencing Player Fantasy lead-up requirement.

- **AC-PW-BARRAGE-UNIFORM-TIER (R7 B11 — NEW; RC-F Pillar 2 dual-channel binding; closes within-barrage lean-tier spread gap; R9 R10a — 2026-06-16 class-3 structurally-mandatory carve-out per level-designer F1B + game-designer F4 convergence)** — **Gate**: BLOCKING at story-Done once Wave Spawner GDD + pattern pool are authored; until then ADVISORY (forward contract on a not-yet-written downstream GDD; same precondition class as AC-PW-WAVE-SPAWNER-PEAK-VELOCITY-FLOOR). **Classification**: Static cross-pattern consistency check (cook-time). **GIVEN** any Wave Spawner barrage pattern (M ≥ 2 members emitted within `BARRAGE_SIMULTANEITY_WINDOW_S`), **WHEN** cook-time tooling reads each member's authored `(SourceLane, TargetLane)` pair and derives `lean_magnitude_tier = abs(TargetLane - SourceLane)` AND derives the barrage's K_class taxonomy bucket per AC-PW-PILLAR-2-BARRAGE-SPATIAL-K's class enumeration, **THEN** all members share identical `lean_magnitude_tier` (integer equality) — **EXCEPT for K_class == 3 barrages (sole admissible triplet `{0, 2, 4}` per AC-PW-PILLAR-2-BARRAGE-SPATIAL-K sub-check (iv)), which MAY admit mixed-tier members per the R8 RC-C class-3 B11 relaxation contract**. The class-3 carve-out is structurally mandatory (R9 R10a — level-designer F1B + game-designer F4 convergence 2026-06-12 R9): under combined R7 B8 (consecutive-target-triplet exclusion) + R7 B11 (uniform-tier) + R7 B12 (distinct-source) the only uniform-tier authorable class-3 configuration is the tier-0 triplet at `{0,2,4}` (sources `{0,2,4}` distinct ✓; any uniform tier ≥ 1 collides under B12 because the three target lanes map to at most two distinct source lanes per the source-distinct constraint); without this carve-out class-3 collapses to K=1 patterns, failing AC-PW-PILLAR-2-BARRAGE-SPATIAL-K sub-check (iii) `MIN_PATTERNS_PER_K_CLASS = 2` and forcing K_class taxonomy re-derivation. Tooling log includes `BARRAGE_TIER_UNIFORM: pattern=NAME tier=T ✓` for non-class-3 barrages AND `BARRAGE_TIER_CLASS3_MIXED_OK: pattern=NAME triplet={0,2,4} tiers={T_member1,T_member2,T_member3} K_class=3 ✓` for class-3 mixed-tier barrages. Failure on a non-class-3 mixed-tier barrage halts admission with error referencing the Channel-separation rationale (mixed-tier barrage breaks dual-channel read outside the class-3 structurally-mandatory exception). Class-3 mixed-tier admission is the Pillar 2 trade adjudicated at R8 RC-C (mixed-tier degrades the collective dual-channel angle-magnitude read for class-3 specifically; each individual member still encodes angle = magnitude correctly at the per-wave level; only the unified-barrage-tier batch-read cue is sacrificed) — preserved at R9 R10a. Distinct barrage patterns within the same phase pool MAY use different uniform tiers (subject to the class-3 carve-out) — the constraint is within-barrage only.

- **AC-PW-BARRAGE-DISTINCT-SOURCE-LANES (R7 B12 — NEW; RC-F Pillar 5 binding; closes source-lane multiplicity gap)** — **Gate**: BLOCKING at story-Done once Wave Spawner GDD + pattern pool are authored; until then ADVISORY (forward contract on a not-yet-written downstream GDD; same precondition class as AC-PW-WAVE-SPAWNER-PEAK-VELOCITY-FLOOR). **Classification**: Static cross-pattern consistency check (cook-time). **GIVEN** any Wave Spawner barrage pattern (M ≥ 2 members), **WHEN** cook-time tooling reads each member's authored `SourceLane`, **THEN** all members' `SourceLane` values are pairwise distinct (no two members share the same source lane). Tooling log includes `BARRAGE_SOURCE_DISTINCT: pattern=NAME source_lanes_distinct=true ✓`. Failure halts admission with error referencing Pillar 5 SPAWNED-frame visual pile rationale (multiple waves at one source lane create unreadable threat piles at LEANING onset). Note: this constraint is on SOURCE lanes; `MIN_BARRAGE_LANE_SEPARATION = 1` (R2 Cluster E semantic) is the parallel constraint on TARGET lanes — both must hold independently.

- **AC-PW-PEAK-NO-ADJACENT-CLUSTER (R7 B8 — NEW R7; R7-B8; gate-promoted ADVISORY → BLOCKING by 2026-06-11 cross-system survivability coordination Option (c); RC-D Pillar 5 + REACTION_BUDGET literature-class closure + R9 cross-system survivability binding)** — **Gate**: **BLOCKING** (R9 coordination 2026-06-11 — promoted from R7 ADVISORY-until-WS-authored to BLOCKING because the F-BARRAGE-SURVIVABILITY-INVARIANT closure depends on this exclusion; reversing this AC re-opens the survivability cliff at REACT=0.25 safe-range ceiling). **Classification**: Static cook-time check. **GIVEN** Wave Spawner PEAK pattern pool's `is_barrage=true` patterns; M=3 barrages, **WHEN** cook-time tooling enumerates each M=3 triplet's three target lane indices, **THEN** no triplet equals `{0,1,2}`, `{1,2,3}`, or `{2,3,4}` (the three all-adjacent-cluster configurations on a 5-lane track). Tooling log includes `PEAK_NO_ADJ_CLUSTER: peak_M3_count=N adj_cluster_count=0 ✓` AND `PEAK_SURVIVING_TRIPLETS: count=7 list={0,1,3},{0,1,4},{0,2,3},{0,2,4},{0,3,4},{1,2,4},{1,3,4}`. Failure halts admission with error referencing F-BARRAGE-SURVIVABILITY-INVARIANT REACTION_BUDGET literature-class binding AND the R9 cross-system survivability binding (worst-case escape at REACT=0.25 ceiling depends on the 7-surviving-triplet enumeration). **Dual binding rationale**: (1) Solo-stimulus reaction-time literature (Carpenter 1988, Hick 1952, Welford 1980, Wood & Zaichkowsky 2002, Mathôt 2014) does not apply to all-adjacent-cluster M=3 PEAK barrages because Hick's Law load at three concurrent stimuli in adjacent perceptual span adds ~50-80ms over the cited budget. (2) F-BARRAGE-SURVIVABILITY-INVARIANT at REACT=0.25 ceiling requires `MIN_ESCAPE_SLIPS = 2`; the 7-surviving-triplet enumeration (creative-director 2026-06-11) confirms worst-case escape = 2 slips across the 7 × 5 = 35 (triplet, player-lane) combinations. The exclusion preserves choice-reaction load ≤2 stimuli in the player's immediate decision space AND keeps the survivability invariant satisfiable across the full REACT safe range. **K_class impact (R7 B8 + R9 coord)**: classes (1) `consecutive-cluster-flee-opposite-edge` (containing `{0,1,2}` and `{2,3,4}`) and (2) `center-cluster-flee-any-edge` (containing `{1,2,3}`) are RETIRED by this AC; classes 3, 4, 5, 6 in the AC-PW-PILLAR-2-BARRAGE-SPATIAL-K taxonomy remain admissible (4 classes covering 7 of the C(5,3)=10 raw triplets). AC-PW-PILLAR-2-BARRAGE-SPATIAL-K K_class floor stands at ≥4 (matches admissible ceiling — see that AC's R7 B8 note + R8 RC-C min_patterns_per_class extension). The R3 CD adjudication #5 strengthening (≥6 was chosen to ensure ≥6 distinct slip-chain decisions) is preserved in spirit because the 4 remaining classes encode 4 distinct escape decisions; the retired classes are excluded for survivability + literature-class reasons, not K_class diversity reasons.

- **AC-PW-OPENER-TIER0-INTRO (R6 B2 — NEW; R8 RC-D 2026-06-11 — 3-part teaching contract extended; RC-B Pillar 2 binding + 3-way Pillar 2 dual-channel teaching binding)** — **Gate**: BLOCKING at story-Done once Wave Spawner GDD + pattern pool are authored; until then ADVISORY (forward contract on a not-yet-written downstream GDD; same precondition class as AC-PW-WAVE-SPAWNER-PEAK-VELOCITY-FLOOR). **Classification**: Static cook-time check. **GIVEN** Wave Spawner OPENER pattern pool + authored run schedule + player spawn lane constant (`PLAYER_SPAWN_LANE = 2` for center-spawn convention), **WHEN** cook-time tooling enumerates the OPENER pool AND simulates the OPENER wave-emission order for the canonical run schedule, **THEN** four checks pass (R8 RC-D extends R6 B2 from 2 to 4 sub-checks): (a) **Tier-0 pool presence**: at least one OPENER-pool pattern satisfies `SourceLane == TargetLane` (tier-0 / straight-forward); tooling log includes `OPENER_TIER0_POOL_PRESENT: tier0_pattern_count=K ≥ 1 ✓`; (b) **Tier-0 first-encounter ordering (R8 RC-D — TIGHTENED 5 → 3)**: at least one of the first **`OPENER_TIER0_INTRO_MAX_WAVE_INDEX = 3`** OPENER waves emitted by the canonical schedule is a tier-0 pattern; tooling log includes `OPENER_TIER0_INTRO: first3_tier0_count=M ≥ 1 ✓`. **R8 RC-D tightening rationale**: at the prior max-index 5, the player has already inferred "rotation = telegraph" encoding from the prior 4 tier-1+ waves before tier-0 arrives; tier-0's zero-rotation boundary case lands too late to teach the dual-channel separation. Tightening to ≤3 forces tier-0 introduction within the first 3 OPENER encounters — before the rotation-as-commitment model fully crystallizes; (c) **Tier-0 lane-placement constraint (R8 RC-D — NEW 2026-06-11)**: the introducing tier-0 pattern (the one satisfying check (b)) MUST have `TargetLane == PLAYER_SPAWN_LANE` (configurable as `OPENER_TIER0_INTRO_TARGET_LANE`). Tooling log includes `OPENER_TIER0_INTRO_LANE_PLACEMENT: introducing_tier0_target_lane=L == PLAYER_SPAWN_LANE=2 ✓`. **R8 RC-D rationale**: a tier-0 wave on a non-player lane reads as "wave is committing somewhere else" — the player can stand still and watch it pass, never having to read the brightness-only signal that teaches the dual-channel encoding. The introducing tier-0 must demand player escape (target the player's lane) so brightness ramp = "this is committing on you, you must move" becomes UN-AVOIDABLE; (d) **Waves 1–2 tier-1 establishment + non-player-lane targeting (R8 RC-D — NEW 2026-06-11; R9 R10a — 2026-06-16 extended with non-player-lane constraint per game-designer F1 + ux-designer F2 path (i) convergence)**: the first two OPENER waves of the canonical schedule MUST be solo tier-1 patterns (`is_barrage == false` AND `|TargetLane - SourceLane| == 1`) **AND target a lane other than the player's spawn lane (`TargetLane != PLAYER_SPAWN_LANE`)**. Tooling log includes `OPENER_WAVES_1_2_TIER1: wave1_tier=1 wave2_tier=1 ✓` AND `OPENER_WAVES_1_2_NOT_PLAYER_LANE: wave1_target=L1!=PLAYER_SPAWN_LANE wave2_target=L2!=PLAYER_SPAWN_LANE ✓`. **R8 RC-D rationale (tier-1 establishment)**: the tier-0 boundary case at wave ≤3 is meaningful only after the player has formed a tier-1 prior (visible angular rotation = visible commitment). If wave 1 or wave 2 were tier-0 (zero rotation), the player has no rotation-magnitude prior to interpret tier-0's zero-rotation case against — the teaching signal is reversed. **R9 R10a rationale (non-player-lane targeting, added 2026-06-16)**: without the additional `TargetLane != PLAYER_SPAWN_LANE` constraint, sub-check (c) (tier-0 introducer at wave ≤3 must target `PLAYER_SPAWN_LANE`) AND sub-check (d) (waves 1–2 tier-1) interact incoherently when the tier-1 patterns happen to also target `PLAYER_SPAWN_LANE` — the player observes three consecutive waves all landing on their own lane and forms the useless heuristic "every wave comes at me" rather than learning that lean direction encodes destination. Constraining waves 1–2 to target a non-player lane forces the player to observe lean → wave goes elsewhere (lean-direction-as-destination teaching), so the tier-0 wave at sub-check (c) targeting `PLAYER_SPAWN_LANE` becomes the meaningful contrast: "brightness ramp without rotation = wave is on YOUR lane already, you must escape." This is the cheaper-than-cook-time-player-position-simulation path R9 ux-designer F2 path (i) prescribed; the alternative path (simulate player lane through the schedule and validate per-wave whether the player would have moved by the time the wave lands) was rejected as out of scope for cook-time tooling. Failure on any of (a)/(b)/(c)/(d) halts admission with error referencing the Channel-separation rationale + 3-way Pillar 2 teaching binding (R8 RC-D 3-way convergence: game-designer + level-designer + art-director) + R9 R10a non-player-lane convergence (game-designer F1 + ux-designer F2 path (i)).

- **AC-PW-PEAK-BARRAGE-MIN-TIER (R8 RC-E — NEW 2026-06-11; ease-curve onset-delay survivability scope binding; R10d — FLOOR=0.65→0.70s context update 2026-06-17)** — **Gate**: BLOCKING at story-Done once Wave Spawner GDD + pattern pool are authored; until then ADVISORY (forward contract on a not-yet-written downstream GDD; same precondition class as AC-PW-WAVE-SPAWNER-PEAK-VELOCITY-FLOOR). **Classification**: Static cook-time check. **GIVEN** Wave Spawner PEAK pattern pool's `is_barrage = true` patterns, **WHEN** cook-time tooling reads each barrage member's authored `(SourceLane, TargetLane)` pair and derives `lean_magnitude_tier = abs(TargetLane - SourceLane)`, **THEN** every PEAK barrage member's `lean_magnitude_tier ≥ 2`. Tooling log includes `PEAK_BARRAGE_MIN_TIER: min_tier=T ≥ 2 ✓`. Failure halts admission with error referencing EC-EASE-CURVE-ONSET-DELAY and F-BARRAGE-SURVIVABILITY-INVARIANT scope clarification. Rationale: under the back-loaded `LeanEaseCurve_Canonical`, tier-1 (1-lane shift, max angle 17°) reaches the direction-detection (1°) perceptibility threshold at lean_progress ≈ 0.30 (≈0.21s at FLOOR=0.70s — re-derived from the pre-R10d ≈0.27s reference at FLOOR=0.65s for the 3° magnitude-discrimination model; under R10d's binding direction-detection model the tier-1 onset is later proportional to the lower angle ceiling). Under R10d FLOOR=0.70s + direction-detection: tier-1 survivability `0.21 + REACT + SLIP_TWEEN × MIN_ESCAPE_SLIPS = 0.21 + 0.25 + 0.30 = 0.76 > 0.70s` — tier-1 PEAK barrages still fail by 60ms at REACT=0.25 ceiling under R10d. The exclusion is independent of the R10d FLOOR raise (tier-1's smaller max-angle reaches direction-detection too late even under R10d's wider budget). Tier-1 SOLO waves and tier-1 OPENER/MID barrages remain permitted — solo waves have no concurrent-stimulus pressure (MIN_SOLO_ESCAPE_SLIPS=1 + smaller per-wave budget); OPENER/MID cadence allows wider per-wave reaction windows. The exclusion is scoped specifically to PEAK barrages where the F-BARRAGE-SURVIVABILITY-INVARIANT applies. **F-BARRAGE-SURVIVABILITY-INVARIANT closure dependency**: the invariant's scope to `lean_magnitude_tier ≥ 2` depends on this AC; relaxation re-opens tier-1 survivability cliff (independent of the R10d FLOOR raise).

- **AC-PW-TIER1-PERCEPTIBILITY (R8 RC-E — NEW 2026-06-11; tier-1 solo survivability bound; R10b gate-level reconcile 2026-06-16; R10d FLOOR raised 0.65 → 0.70s 2026-06-17 — closure now holds under BOTH perceptibility models at design-time; advisor-flagged 2026-06-17 — Alpha-gate label retained as BLOCKING with R10d semantic clarification, not downgraded)** — **Gate**: **ADVISORY at story-Done** (the cross-system arithmetic table below MUST be derivable; under R10d FLOOR=0.70s closure holds under BOTH perceptibility models so the verification is fully observable at story-Done — no PROTO-GATE-gated branch remains); **BLOCKING at Alpha (R10d semantic clarification)** (PROTO-GATE perceptibility-model confidence check must be exercised at Alpha; the BLOCKING gate fires on FAILURE TO RUN the perceptibility validation, NOT on F-invariant closure conditioning — F-invariant closes at design-time under R10d FLOOR raise independent of PROTO-GATE outcome; if PROTO-GATE empirically reveals magnitude-discrimination is operative rather than direction-detection, that constitutes a prototype-found defect class triggering a separate CD ruling at the Alpha gate per the EC-EASE-CURVE-ONSET-DELAY R10d framing, not a direct closure-block on THIS AC's story-Done verification). **Gate-level R10d reconcile rationale (2026-06-17, advisor-corrected)**: under R10d FLOOR=0.70s + registry-locked SLIP_TWEEN=0.15 + MIN_SOLO_ESCAPE_SLIPS=1, the verification table closes at REACT=0.25 ceiling under BOTH the direction-detection (1°) and magnitude-discrimination (3°) perceptibility models — the R10d FLOOR raise absorbed the magnitude-model REACT=0.25 ceiling cliff that R10b's BLOCKING-at-Alpha framing was designed to gate. The story-Done gate remains ADVISORY (static verification observable) and the Alpha gate remains BLOCKING (must-run prototype validation) but with R10d's semantic reframe: BLOCKING-at-Alpha is about exercising the perceptibility-model confidence check, not gating F-invariant closure. CD ruling Section 4 #6 said "reword the AC so the invariant is no longer conditioned on the prototype outcome" — R10d's semantic clarification honors that without authorizing an Alpha-label downgrade. **Classification**: Static analysis (cook-time + cross-system arithmetic verification at story-Done; PROTO-GATE confidence check at Alpha is separate). **GIVEN** tier-1 solo waves (`is_barrage = false` AND `|TargetLane - SourceLane| = 1`), the per-wave survivability budget is `t_perceptible_onset_tier1 + REACTION_BUDGET + SLIP_TWEEN × MIN_SOLO_ESCAPE_SLIPS ≤ TELEGRAPH_WINDOW_FLOOR_S`, where `MIN_SOLO_ESCAPE_SLIPS = 1` (solo wave requires only 1 slip to escape) and `t_perceptible_onset_tier1` is the eased-curve time to reach the operative perceptibility threshold at tier-1's 17° max angle. **WHEN** reviewer verifies the bound at REACT safe-range endpoints under both perceptibility models against R10d FLOOR=0.70s, **THEN** invariant holds at both REACT endpoints under both perceptibility models. **Verification table (R10d FLOOR=0.70s)**:

  | Perceptibility model | t_perceptible_onset_tier1 | At REACT=0.20 | At REACT=0.25 |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | Magnitude-discrimination (3° threshold) | `~0.27s` (lean_progress ≈ 0.41 at tier-1's 17° max) | `0.27 + 0.20 + 0.15 = 0.62s ≤ 0.70s` ✓ 80ms margin (widened from 30ms under pre-R10d FLOOR=0.65s) | `0.27 + 0.25 + 0.15 = 0.67s ≤ 0.70s` ✓ 30ms margin (was FAIL by 20ms under pre-R10d FLOOR=0.65s — R10d closes this cliff) |
