  | Direction-detection (1° threshold) | `~0.15s` (lean_progress ≈ 0.23 at tier-1's 17° max) | `0.15 + 0.20 + 0.15 = 0.50s ≤ 0.70s` ✓ 200ms margin (widened from 150ms under pre-R10d FLOOR=0.65s) | `0.15 + 0.25 + 0.15 = 0.55s ≤ 0.70s` ✓ 150ms margin (widened from 100ms under pre-R10d FLOOR=0.65s) |

  **R10d closure condition (updated)**: tier-1 solo waves survive across the full REACT safe range [0.20, 0.25]s under BOTH perceptibility models under FLOOR=0.70s — the magnitude-discrimination row's 30ms margin at REACT=0.25 ceiling is the narrowest case but still PASSES. The pre-R10d "IF AND ONLY IF direction-detection" conditional closure is RETIRED — closure no longer depends on PROTO-GATE's empirical determination. PROTO-GATE remains a confidence check on the binding direction-detection design decision but is no longer load-bearing for THIS AC's closure. Tooling log includes `TIER1_PERCEPT_DIRECTION_MODEL_R10d: 0.15+0.25+0.15=0.55 ≤ 0.70 ✓ 150ms margin` and `TIER1_PERCEPT_MAGNITUDE_MODEL_R10d: 0.27+0.25+0.15=0.67 ≤ 0.70 ✓ 30ms margin (closes under R10d FLOOR raise)`. **Cross-reference**: tier-1 OPENER/MID barrages — see future Wave Spawner GDD's cadence analysis; survivability budget under OPENER/MID per-wave-interval is larger than PEAK's TELEGRAPH_WINDOW_FLOOR_S baseline, but exact OPENER/MID survivability budgets are owned by Wave Spawner.

- **AC-PW-TIER-DIRECTION-DETECTION-PROTO-GATE (R8 RC-E — NEW 2026-06-11; ease-curve perceptibility validation gate; R10b methodology rewrite 2026-06-16 — reaction-time paradigm replaced with two-alternative forced-choice adaptive staircase; R10d 2026-06-17 — role reframed from load-bearing closure gate to perceptibility-model confidence check; FLOOR=0.65→0.70s rendering update; advisor-flagged 2026-06-17 — Alpha-gate label retained as BLOCKING with R10d semantic clarification, not downgraded; R11a-measurement 2026-06-18 — apparatus specification locked (B6), trial randomization protocol specified (B7), Alpha failure escalation paths pre-authorized (B8) superseding R10d inline escalation list)** — **Gate**: ADVISORY at story-Done; **BLOCKING at Alpha (R10d semantic clarification, not downgrade)** — PROTO-GATE was R8 RC-E's load-bearing closure mechanism for F-BARRAGE-SURVIVABILITY-INVARIANT under pre-R10d FLOOR=0.65s; under R10d FLOOR=0.70s the invariant closes on the FLOOR raise alone — see EC-EASE-CURVE-ONSET-DELAY and AC-PW-TIER1-PERCEPTIBILITY R10d entries — and PROTO-GATE's role is reframed as the perceptibility-model **confidence check** on the binding direction-detection design decision rather than the closure gate. The Alpha gate remains BLOCKING (the perceptibility validation MUST be exercised at Alpha — "we still want the 1° threshold empirically confirmed" per CD ruling); the semantic shift is that BLOCKING applies to must-run prototype validation rather than F-invariant closure-conditioning. The empirical determination remains valuable for design confidence and for early detection of an Alpha-phase magnitude-discrimination-operative defect class; its outcome no longer gates F-invariant or AC-PW-TIER1-PERCEPTIBILITY closure under R10d, but a confirmed-magnitude-discrimination outcome triggers a separate CD ruling at the Alpha gate per the EC-EASE-CURVE-ONSET-DELAY R10d framing. **Classification**: Manual on-device perceptibility playtest (two-alternative forced-choice psychometric methodology) — confidence check on the R10d-binding direction-detection design decision. **GIVEN** a minimum-spec mobile device + 6-inch screen under the following locked apparatus conditions (R11a-measurement — 2026-06-18, per Pull-Wave R11 ux-designer B6 finding): **Apparatus Specification**: viewing distance **35–40cm** (tighter than the gameplay arm's-length target — the staircase apparatus requires a reproducible retinal-projection band; at 30cm a 1° lean subtends ~5.2mm on a 6-inch screen; at 50cm it subtends ~8.7mm — 67% variance in retinal projection size that would dominate the 0.15s threshold measurement; tester holds device at arm's length in portrait orientation and adjusts to the specified band, confirmed with a ruler before each session); display brightness **80%** (device-locked for the staircase session — per-device backlight variance is capped by fixing brightness; testers confirm the brightness setting before each session); ambient lighting **~500 lux ±100 lux** (standard office lighting condition; target with a lux meter before each session; avoid windows or direct overhead lights creating hotspots on screen); **True Tone disabled** (True Tone warms display color temperature and shifts perceived contrast on the OPENER/MID/PEAK lean brightness tells — disable in iOS Display Settings before session start; Android equivalent adaptive display modes disabled); display orientation **portrait** (per game spec); **same device per tester across all 4 tiers** (within-subject device consistency — variance across testers is captured by the 3-tester n, not within a tester's staircase runs). A fresh tester picking up this AC must be able to configure the test rig from this specification alone without ambiguity. The eased-rotation animation driven by `LeanEaseCurve_Canonical` control points `(0.0→0.0, 0.4→0.15, 0.7→0.7, 1.0→1.0)` under R10b-pinned cubic Hermite interpolation, AND wave samples at tier-1 (17° max), tier-2 (22° max), tier-3 (25° max), tier-4 (28° max) leans rendered at FLOOR = 0.70s LeanDurationS (R10d update — was FLOOR=0.65s under pre-R10d). **WHEN** 3 testers each complete one adaptive staircase per tier (12 staircases total — 4 tiers × 3 testers; ≥30 trials or 10 reversals per staircase, whichever comes first, preserving the original 360-trial total budget). **Trial protocol (two-alternative forced-choice, per R10b ux-designer F1 path (c) — 2026-06-16)**: each trial renders a single lean stimulus for a controlled duration `t_stimulus` (the staircase variable; starting value `t_stimulus = 0.20s`, step size `0.01s`, floor `0.05s`, ceiling `0.30s`); at the end of `t_stimulus` the rotation freezes at its eased-curve value and holds for 0.10s of observation; the tester is then prompted "lean direction: LEFT or RIGHT?" **with NO time pressure** and reports the perceived direction; the response is scored against ground truth and the staircase advances per the 2-down-1-up rule (two consecutive correct → decrease `t_stimulus` by step; one wrong → increase `t_stimulus` by step), converging on the 70.7% direction-discrimination accuracy threshold (the closest standard staircase target to the 75% psychophysics convention). The per-tester per-tier perceptibility-threshold `t_threshold(tier, tester)` is the mean of the last 6 staircase reversals. **Trial randomization (R11a-measurement — 2026-06-18, per Pull-Wave R11 ux-designer B7 finding)**: within each staircase the LEFT/RIGHT trial sequence MUST follow a **pre-seeded 50/50 balanced pseudo-random shuffle** — NOT an independent Bernoulli draw per trial. Independent Bernoulli per-trial admits runs of 4–5 consecutive same-direction trials; repeated same-direction runs allow motor-anticipation bias to develop, inflating the apparent correct-response rate above the true perceptibility threshold and corrupting the staircase convergence. Protocol: (a) pre-generate a balanced sequence of exactly `[trial_budget]` labels (50% LEFT, 50% RIGHT) by shuffling a half-half list; (b) apply a **no-runs-of-3** constraint — if the shuffle produces 3 or more consecutive same-direction trials at any position, reshuffle the sequence (repeat until the constraint is met); (c) record the random seed used for reproducibility — log seed alongside the staircase trajectory in the evidence file; (d) apply the same protocol independently for each of the 12 staircases (4 tiers × 3 testers); no sequence is reused across staircases. This protocol is the same across all 4 tiers and all 3 testers. Log line per staircase: `TRIAL_RAND_SEED: tier=T tester=N seed=XXXXXXXX balance=50/50 max_run=2 ✓`. **THEN** the per-tier median (across 3 testers) of `t_threshold` MUST satisfy two checks: (a) **Direction-detection threshold floor (the binding case)**: `median(t_threshold(tier-1)) ≤ 0.15s` — see AC-PW-TIER1-PERCEPTIBILITY for the pre-authorized escalation paths if this fails; tooling log includes `TIER1_t_threshold_median = X.XXs ≤ 0.15s ✓/✗`. (b) **Cross-tier threshold monotonicity** (smaller tier reaches perceptibility threshold later or equal): `median(t_threshold(tier-1)) ≥ median(t_threshold(tier-2)) ≥ median(t_threshold(tier-3)) ≥ median(t_threshold(tier-4))` — higher tiers reach perceptibility earlier per the same eased curve due to larger max angles; tooling log includes `MONOTONICITY: tier1=X.XX ≥ tier2=X.XX ≥ tier3=X.XX ≥ tier4=X.XX ✓/✗`. Failure on (a) under R10d is reframed from "closure-blocking design defect" to **Alpha-phase confidence-check failure**: it indicates the binding direction-detection design decision is empirically incorrect (magnitude-discrimination is operative at tier-1) and constitutes a prototype-found defect class to be addressed via a separate CD ruling at the Alpha gate. **Alpha PROTO-GATE Failure Escalation (R11a-measurement — 2026-06-18, per Pull-Wave R11 ux-designer B8 finding; SUPERSEDES the inline escalation list that appeared in the R10d authoring of this AC — the old paths (i) front-load ease curve and (iii) tighten SLIP_TWEEN are retired; the new pre-authorized paths below replace them)**: If `median(t_threshold(tier-1)) > 0.15s` at the Alpha PROTO-GATE, the following pre-authorized escalation paths apply in priority order: PATH (i) — **RECOMMENDED: raise `TELEGRAPH_WINDOW_FLOOR_S` by one increment** per the R10d precedent (CD PATH (i) raised FLOOR from 0.65s → 0.70s; the same mechanism applies if magnitude-discrimination proves operative). The PATH (i) lower bound is **0.82s**: under the magnitude-discrimination (3°) model at REACT=0.25 ceiling, `t_perceptible_onset + REACT + SLIP_TWEEN × MIN_ESCAPE_SLIPS = 0.27s + 0.25s + 0.30s = 0.82s` (derivable from AC-PW-TIER1-PERCEPTIBILITY magnitude-discrimination row at REACT=0.25 ceiling, the narrowest case; **R12 qa-lead notation fix 2026-06-18 — was `SLIP_TWEEN × MIN_SOLO_ESCAPE_SLIPS = 0.30s`, but `MIN_SOLO_ESCAPE_SLIPS = 1` would yield `0.15 × 1 = 0.15s`, not 0.30s; the 0.30s product is correct under `MIN_ESCAPE_SLIPS = 2` — the barrage-invariant constant. Symbol corrected.**). The PATH (i) upper bound is **0.76s** (R12 systems-designer derivation 2026-06-18): bounded by Constraint A (Path B + 0.12s overlap cascade): under the R11a-binding 0.12s intra-phase overlap window, `MID_key = FLOOR + 0.12` and `OPENER_key = FLOOR + 0.24`. To keep `OPENER_key ≤ 1.0s` (practical "generous-but-not-lethargic" perceptibility ceiling — above 1.0s a leaning voxel mass becomes indistinct as a signal against ambient track motion on a 6-inch screen), `FLOOR ≤ 0.76s`. At FLOOR = 0.76s: OPENER = 1.0s, MID = 0.88s, PEAK = 0.76s. Constraint B (PEAK spawn-interval floor at `wave_spawn_interval_s = 1.0s`): `FLOOR < 1.0s` (above which telegraph window equals/exceeds inter-spawn gap — continuous overlap with zero player rest, Pillar 3 / Pillar 5 violation). Constraint A is the binding ceiling. Current FLOOR=0.70s has **60ms headroom** before the Constraint A ceiling. Raising above 0.76s requires either CD sign-off on OPENER telegraph window > 1.0s (Pillar 3 trade) OR revising the 0.12s overlap window itself (Path C reauthoring), and either requires fresh CD adjudication. PATH (i) is preferred because it is a single-parameter raise on a DPC-owned value with smallest blast radius, following the R10d precedent. **PATH (i) iteration cap (R12 ux-designer BLOCKING-3 closure 2026-06-18 + CD R12 ruling)**: **only ONE PATH (i) FLOOR raise is authorized** per PROTO-GATE failure; if a second PROTO-GATE confidence-check fails after a single PATH (i) raise has been applied, the team MUST escalate to PATH (ii) or PATH (iii) before any further PATH (i) iteration. The cap exists to prevent indefinite FLOOR raises with diminishing returns while deferring the underlying design question; the cap is binding regardless of remaining headroom under the 0.76s ceiling. PATH (ii) — **CD-handoff: enlarge lean angular magnitude per tier** (revise tier angle schedule, e.g., 17°/22°/25°/28° → enlarged spread). This is a Pillar 3 trade (changes the lean-visual character of all tiers) requiring creative-director adjudication before adoption; pre-authorized here as a CD-handoff path so R12 planning does not require a new routing decision. PATH (iii) — **fail-safe: if both PATH (i) and PATH (ii) are blocked**, ship tier discrimination as ADVISORY for tiers above tier-1. Under this path, tier-1 direction-detection is conceded to be marginal; the cadence change between OPENER/MID/PEAK is still felt by the player via wave velocity and frequency even if fine per-tier angle discrimination is not reliable. **R12 qa-lead BLOCKING-2 closure 2026-06-18**: the prior PATH (iii) text "MID and PEAK tier discrimination gates downgraded from BLOCKING to ADVISORY" referenced ACs (`AC-PW-MID-TIER-DISCRIMINATION` / `AC-PW-PEAK-TIER-DISCRIMINATION`) that do not exist in this GDD — paper-only-AC class. Concrete restatement: under PATH (iii), the **sub-check (b) cross-tier threshold monotonicity** of THIS AC (AC-PW-TIER-DIRECTION-DETECTION-PROTO-GATE — `median(t_threshold(tier-1)) ≥ median(t_threshold(tier-2)) ≥ median(t_threshold(tier-3)) ≥ median(t_threshold(tier-4))`) is downgraded from a BLOCKING-at-Alpha gate condition to ADVISORY-at-Alpha (the staircase data is still collected and logged for post-ship analysis, but a monotonicity violation no longer blocks the Alpha ship). The sub-check (a) direction-detection threshold floor (`median(t_threshold(tier-1)) ≤ 0.15s`) remains BLOCKING-at-Alpha — PATH (iii) does not concede tier-1 itself; only the per-tier ordering above tier-1. A post-ship tuning pass tracks the deferred monotonicity check. These escalations are NOT design-time hedges or pre-authored branches — R10d's binding direction-detection decision means design-time closure does not depend on PROTO-GATE's outcome, and these paths are only relevant if the Alpha confidence check fails. Evidence saved to `production/qa/evidence/ac-pw-tier-direction-detection-[date]-[device].md` with per-tester per-tier full staircase trajectories (`t_stimulus` value + correct/wrong response per trial), the last-6-reversal mean `t_threshold` estimate, AND the per-tier 3-tester median used for the pass-condition checks. **R10d rationale (2026-06-17)**: under the R10d FLOOR raise (0.65 → 0.70s), F-BARRAGE-SURVIVABILITY-INVARIANT and AC-PW-TIER1-PERCEPTIBILITY both close at design-time under the binding direction-detection model AND retain a (smaller) margin under the magnitude-discrimination model — so the AC's role shifts from "closure gate that the invariant depends on" to "confidence check that confirms the design-time perceptibility-model assumption." The R8 RC-E "residual gap admitted as prototype risk" framing is retired by R10d; the residual surface is now an Alpha-phase prototype-found defect class, not a design-time admission. **R10b methodology rationale (2026-06-16, per R9 qa-lead F5 + ux-designer F1 path (c))**: the prior reaction-time paradigm (`p95(t_decision) ≤ 0.15s`) was mathematically incoherent — `t_decision` measures elapsed time from `lean_progress=0` to button press, conflating perceptibility-onset latency AND motor reaction time. Demanding `t_decision ≤ 0.15s` while `REACTION_BUDGET = 0.20s` (motor reaction time floor) is logically impossible: motor reaction alone exceeds the bound before perceptibility is even considered. Two-alternative forced-choice (2-AFC) with adaptive staircase **decouples** perceptibility-onset latency from motor reaction time: the stimulus is presented for a controlled duration `t_stimulus`; the response is collected without time pressure; only the stimulus duration enters the threshold estimate. The resulting `t_threshold` is the pure perceptibility-onset latency, directly comparable to `t_perceptible_direction` in EC-EASE-CURVE-ONSET-DELAY. The 2-AFC paradigm is the gold standard for perceptibility-floor measurement in psychophysics literature precisely because it isolates perceptibility from motor + decision noise; the prior RT paradigm was a measurement-design defect. Trial budget preserved at 360 trials total via per-staircase trial cap; only the methodology changed.

- **AC-PW-LANE-ENUM-CONSISTENCY (R1 RC-F sys-design B-3 fix)** — **Gate**: BLOCKING at story-Done. **Classification**: Static cross-GDD + cross-source consistency check. **GIVEN** Pull-Wave Rule 2 binds `NUM_LANES = 5`. **WHEN** reviewer + CI grep verifies (i) PM GDD Rule 1 reflects 5-lane track (post-`/propagate-design-change`); (ii) `EPlayerLane` enum (or replacement `int32` lane index) in PM source enumerates lanes 0–4 (5 members) with no Left/Center/Right legacy values; (iii) `LANE_OFFSET_CM` legacy constant is removed OR documented as deprecated; (iv) Pull-Wave + Wave Spawner + Collision sources all reference the same lane-count constant (either `NUM_LANES` shared header or `EPlayerLane::Num` equivalent). **THEN** all four sub-checks pass. CI grep target: `rg -n 'EPlayerLane::(Left|Center|Right)' src/ | wc -l` returns 0. If PM revision has not landed, this AC blocks Pull-Wave story-Done — `/propagate-design-change` for PM is prerequisite.

### Cross-System Consistency

- **AC-PW-SLIP-TWEEN-CONSISTENCY** — **Gate**: BLOCKING at story-Done. **Classification**: Static cross-GDD consistency check. **GIVEN** this GDD's F-BARRAGE-SURVIVABILITY-INVARIANT binds PM's `SLIP_TWEEN_DURATION_S` to `[0.10, 0.15]s` (range preserved under R2 Cluster E path (b) resolution; would collapse to `{0.10s}` under path (a) — not chosen). **WHEN** reviewer reads both this GDD AND PM GDD's Tuning Knobs for `SLIP_TWEEN_DURATION_S`. **THEN** PM GDD authored safe range matches `[0.10, 0.15]s`. If PM still records `[0.10, 0.25]s`, AC FAILS — PM revision via `/propagate-design-change` is prerequisite to Pull-Wave story-Done.

- **AC-PW-WAVE-SPAWNER-PEAK-VELOCITY-FLOOR (R1 RC-D fix)** — **Gate**: BLOCKING at story-Done once Wave Spawner GDD is authored; until then ADVISORY (forward contract on a not-yet-written downstream GDD). **Classification**: Static cross-GDD consistency check + cook-time pool-average tooling. **GIVEN** Wave Spawner GDD's per-phase pattern pools authored with per-pattern `ForwardVelocityMs`. **WHEN** cook-time tooling computes `peak_avg = mean(ForwardVelocityMs over PEAK pool)` AND `opener_avg = mean(ForwardVelocityMs over OPENER pool)`. **THEN** `peak_avg ≥ 1.5 × opener_avg`. Tooling log includes `PEAK_VEL_RATIO: peak/opener = X.XX ≥ 1.50`. Failure means PEAK pattern pool requires re-authoring before story-Done OR Pull-Wave Rule 7 must be revised to per-phase velocity scaling (would reopen OQ-DPC-2 closed=NO; do not pursue without CD sign-off).

### Performance Budget

- **AC-PW-22a (CPU budget — ADVISORY, on-device; R1 RC-E E-6 fix — mixed-state frame mix; R7 B10 — PEAK-state mix derived mechanically from tuning knobs, not stated as fixed assumption; LEANING fraction recomputed against R10d FLOOR raise 0.65s→0.70s — R11a-arithmetic 2026-06-18; was R2-path-b 0.6→0.65)** — **Gate**: ADVISORY at story-Done; BLOCKING at Alpha. **GIVEN** worst-case 16 simultaneous in-flight waves (DPC cap) in a **PEAK-representative state mix** at 60Hz on minimum-spec mobile device (TBD at prototype milestone, matching DPC AC-22a convention). **R7 B10 state mix derivation (mechanical from tuning knobs — supersedes R1's fixed 2/10/3/1 assumption)**: at PEAK defaults — `TELEGRAPH_WINDOW_FLOOR_S = 0.70s` (R11a-arithmetic 2026-06-18 update per R10d CD ruling path (i); was 0.65s; LEANING duration); `TravelDurationS = SPAWN_PLANE_Z_OFFSET_M / ForwardVelocityMs = 15.0 / 10.5 (PEAK avg velocity at 1.5× OPENER avg 7.0 m/s) ≈ 1.43s` (TRAVERSING duration); `WAVE_DESPAWN_HOLD_S = 0.15s` (LANDED duration); DESPAWNING-state lifetime ≈ 1 tick (16ms ≈ 0.016s). Total wave lifetime ≈ `0.70 + 1.43 + 0.15 + 0.016 = 2.30s` (R11a-arithmetic 2026-06-18 — was 2.25s under pre-R10d FLOOR=0.65s). State fractions: LEANING `0.70/2.30 = 30.4%`; TRAVERSING `1.43/2.30 = 62.2%`; LANDED `0.15/2.30 = 6.5%`; DESPAWNING `0.016/2.30 = 0.7%`. At 16 active waves: expected LEANING count ≈ `16 × 0.304 = 4.87 → 5`; TRAVERSING ≈ `16 × 0.622 = 9.95 → 10`; LANDED ≈ `16 × 0.065 = 1.04 → 1`; DESPAWNING ≈ `16 × 0.007 = 0.11 → 0` (DESPAWNING is one-tick; at PEAK steady-state most frames see 0, occasional frames see 1). **R7-derived PEAK-representative mix: 5 LEANING / 10 TRAVERSING / 1 LANDED / 0-1 DESPAWNING** (unchanged integer counts under R10d FLOOR raise — the LEANING fraction shifts from 29% to 30.4% but the rounded integer-state assignment at 16 waves is invariant under this perturbation; test harness instantiates this mix exactly; the 0-1 DESPAWNING is sampled as 1 to exercise the heaviest case). The R1 fixed mix (2 LEANING / 10 TRAVERSING / 3 LANDED / 1 DESPAWNING) was authored before R2 path-b FLOOR raise and never re-derived; performance-analyst R7 B10 caught the undercount in LEANING (R1's 2 vs R7-derived 5 — LEANING tick body reads `lean_progress`, evaluates `EaseCurve(lean_progress)`, writes `LeanChargeIntensity` to PerInstanceCustomData[0], includes the pause gate check; cost is comparable to TRAVERSING tick body). **WHEN** Pull-Wave tick time profiled via Unreal Insights or `SCOPE_CYCLE_COUNTER(STAT_PullWaveTick)` over 120 frames at the R7-derived mix, **THEN** 95th-percentile per-tick wall-clock for all 16 waves combined ≤ `0.25 ms` provisional. RSM/DPC/PM active; other systems idle. Profiler capture saved to `production/qa/evidence/ac-pw-22a-[date]-[device].png`. **Tuning-dependence note**: if `TELEGRAPH_WINDOW_FLOOR_S` is revised (e.g., DPC GDD raises it), AC-PW-22a state mix MUST be re-derived from the new lifetime fractions before the next budget validation pass. R1-draft TRAVERSING-only scope was insufficient: LANDED-hold tick cost (small but nonzero) + DESPAWNING-entry teardown (Rule 13 ordered pipeline) + LEANING bookkeeping all contribute to real PEAK frame load and must be included in the measurement.
- **AC-PW-22c (R4 RC-R3-6 — NEW; ghost-trail readability + lateral containment, PROVISIONAL-PROTO gate)** — **Gate**: ADVISORY at story-Done; BLOCKING at Alpha. **Classification**: Manual on-device perceptibility check. **GIVEN** a minimum-spec mobile device running Pull-Wave's TRAVERSING visual at PEAK velocity (default `ForwardVelocityMs = 9.0–12.0 m/s` per the 1.5× PEAK floor; Wave Spawner GDD will lock per-pattern values) AND a 6-inch screen at typical mobile viewing distance, **WHEN** 3 testers each observe a sample of ≥20 in-flight waves spanning OPENER (`≈6 m/s`) and PEAK (`≥9 m/s`) velocity tiers, **THEN** both of: (a) **Trail readability**: in ≥90% of observed waves at PEAK velocity, the trail reads as 3 distinct cube ghosts (not a fused stamp or motion blur) — testers must be able to describe the trail as "three boxes behind it" without prompting; (b) **Lateral containment**: in 100% of observed waves, the ghost-trail's lateral world-X extent stays within `LANE_WIDTH_M × 1.25` of the wave's center across the full trajectory — no ghost cube swings into an adjacent lane visually. Failure on (a) at any velocity tier surfaces a technique-revision (e.g., increase ghost-cube spacing, animate cube offset along trajectory per OQ-PW-7 fallback (a)). Failure on (b) blocks Alpha — overshooting trails would mis-cue safe lanes (Pillar 5 read). Evidence saved to `production/qa/evidence/ac-pw-22c-[date]-[device].md` with photo/video at PEAK velocity. Cross-reference: OQ-PW-7 art-director sign-off gate.

- **AC-PW-22b (CI structural grep — BLOCKING; R2 Cluster A 5th pattern added; R4 RC-R3-2 + RC-R3-4 + RC-R3-6 additions for patterns 6–10; R5 B1 — heading "all ten" corrected from stale "all five")** — **GIVEN** Pull-Wave implementation source, **WHEN** source-grep CI runs against `src/gameplay/pull_wave/`, **THEN** all ten patterns match (case-insensitive unless noted):

    2. `SCOPE_CYCLE_COUNTER(STAT_PullWaveTick)` inside `Tick()` body.

    4. `MIN_BARRAGE_LANE_SEPARATION` constant defined in source (not just GDD).

(b) ALLOW — `rg -n 'TraverseElapsedS\s*\+=' src/gameplay/pull_wave/` returns ≥1 match (confirms the accumulator increment exists in source). FORBID failure is a hard regression; ALLOW failure means the accumulator was removed without replacement.

    7. **Cross-knob tier ordering invariant (R4 RC-R3-2; R6 B6 — expanded from single grep to four adjacent-pair greps for full chain coverage; R7 B6 — extended with sub-patterns 7e-7h for MIN_GAP=3.0 defense-in-depth against the art-director R6-flagged adversarial gap-collapse class)**: source includes `static_assert` (or runtime `check`) statements covering both the ordering chain AND the MIN_GAP chain. **Ordering chain (7a-7d, R6 B6)** — full four-term: `LEAN_ANGLE_TIER0_DEG < LEAN_ANGLE_TIER1_DEG && LEAN_ANGLE_TIER1_DEG < LEAN_ANGLE_TIER2_DEG && LEAN_ANGLE_TIER2_DEG < LEAN_ANGLE_TIER3_DEG && LEAN_ANGLE_TIER3_DEG < LEAN_ANGLE_TIER4_DEG`. **MIN_GAP chain (7e-7h, R7 B6 — NEW)** — full four-pair: `(LEAN_ANGLE_TIER1_DEG - LEAN_ANGLE_TIER0_DEG) >= LEAN_ANGLE_MIN_TIER_GAP_DEG && (LEAN_ANGLE_TIER2_DEG - LEAN_ANGLE_TIER1_DEG) >= LEAN_ANGLE_MIN_TIER_GAP_DEG && (LEAN_ANGLE_TIER3_DEG - LEAN_ANGLE_TIER2_DEG) >= LEAN_ANGLE_MIN_TIER_GAP_DEG && (LEAN_ANGLE_TIER4_DEG - LEAN_ANGLE_TIER3_DEG) >= LEAN_ANGLE_MIN_TIER_GAP_DEG`. CI grep requires **all eight** of these patterns to return ≥1 match (sub-patterns 7a-7h). The prior R6 form only enforced ordering and admitted misconfigurations like `TIER3=27, TIER4=28` (both within per-knob safe ranges at R6; ordering 27<28 holds; gap=1° sub-perceptible) — the R7 B6 MIN_GAP sub-patterns close this class.

    12. **Ease curve registry-asset reference enforcement (R7 B7 — NEW)**: FORBID — `rg -n 'UCurveFloat\s*\*\s*\w+\s*=\s*Load' src/gameplay/pull_wave/` returns 0 matches (inlined `UCurveFloat*` direct-load construction in rotation-driver source path; the canonical ease curve MUST be loaded via the registry-driven path, never as an inlined asset reference). ALLOW — `rg -n 'LeanEaseCurve_Canonical|GetEaseCurve_FromRegistry' src/gameplay/pull_wave/` returns ≥1 match (registry-canonical curve name or accessor present). Rationale: R7 B7 promotes the ease curve to a registry-locked named asset with cook-time fingerprint validation; an inlined `UCurveFloat*` member that points to a substituted asset (e.g., a standard cubic ease-in-out preset returning ~0.10 at t=0.4 instead of the canonical 0.15) would silently break the Pillar 2 phase-alignment fix from R2 Cluster F. The FORBID + ALLOW combination ensures the canonical-asset path is in source AND inlined alternatives are absent.

  All twelve required for green CI (patterns 1–11 plus the new R7 B7 pattern 12). Pattern 7 itself decomposes into 8 sub-greps (7a–7h) per R6 B6 + R7 B6. Mirrors DPC AC-22b pattern. The combined FORBID + ALLOW patterns are stronger than either alone: FORBID alone could be satisfied by removing the relevant code entirely; ALLOW alone could be satisfied by keeping both correct and incorrect forms.

Rationale: F-BARRAGE-SURVIVABILITY-INVARIANT computes the escape budget assuming a single `TELEGRAPH_WINDOW_FLOOR_S` covers all barrage onsets; mixed velocities produce staggered TravelDurationS per member, which invalidates the uniform-timing model and lets some barrage members land before the player has resolved the slip chain for others. Decision is at Pull-Wave layer because the survivability invariant is Pull-Wave-owned. |

| **OQ-PW-7** (NEW R1 RC-G G-3; fallback (b) STRIPPED R2 CD adjudication) | **Cutout-ghost trail aesthetic confirmation**: does the alpha-tested voxel-ghost motion trail (R1-revised technique, replacing R1-draft translucent ribbon) read as "approaching motion" rather than "static after-image"? The mobile-tile-GPU shift forced an aesthetic shift toward sharp-edged Pillar-4-consistent voxel ghosts. | art-director + technical-artist | Telegraph + Pull-Wave prototype milestone | Art-director sign-off REQUIRED at prototype. If the cutout ghost reads as static, two fallbacks remain: **(a)** animate per-instance cube offsets along the trajectory to suggest velocity (ISMC-compatible — drives offset via PerInstanceCustomData without breaking the single-batch invariant); **(c)** revisit ribbon technique with aggressive overdraw budget (requires ADR; defers mobile thermal risk). **Fallback (b) "selective translucency for closest 4 waves" is REMOVED (R2 CD adjudication).** Rationale: applying translucency selectively based on distance-rank creates perceptual inconsistency — two visually identical waves at different distances would render differently, undermining Pillar 5 (players cannot rely on appearance to determine threat). The R1 RC-G G-3 BINDING (cutout only, not translucent, not optional) is therefore unconditional; no distance-rank exception exists. Decision documented in art bible. |
